| `DeleteConfirm` · `DetachDefaultConfirm` · two trash modals (names left to implementation — 2nd edition · G11 · G12) | Confirmation **modal** — an **in-app modal that covers the whole window** (behind it the agent keeps running and the chat keeps updating · only input is blocked · B9 · B10 · user 2026-10-19 「ㅇㅋ 그럼 모달로해」 · 「ㅇㅇ 모달로하면되고」). Trash modal = ① empty · permanent-delete confirmation — a delete question that does not mention the trash: title 「영구 삭제」 · body 「항목 N개를 삭제하시겠습니까?」 (N = the number of deleted items (rows) the trash view lists · not the number of bundle folders · empty-all is the same — main 2026-10-31 — M79 · the bundles sent are also only those the view lists — M83) · buttons [Cancel] [Delete] (user 2026-10-31 G50 「그냥 휴지통말고 삭제하시겠습니까해」 — replaces the old 「Windows 휴지통으로 보냅니다 — 안 되면 바로 지웁니다」 (main-session decision 2026-10-20 — say it first, not after) and its draft 「…휴지통이 없는 드라이브면 바로 지웁니다」 (G45 · M59 · tied to U1) · the behavior is unchanged — it is handed to the OS trash, the OS decides, and items the OS refuses and which remain are reported in the result · G47 · §6-5 7) ② restore name collision 「같은 이름이 이미 있습니다」 [Restore under another name] = 「(2)」 · [Cancel] — no overwrite. The delete confirmation (2nd edition) appears only when there are references, and if several were chosen, it carries the references of all items involved in one modal (B7) · for a folder, it merges the references of everything inside · the same confirmation also applies when deleting or renaming a `SKILL.md` of a referenced skill, or moving it out from directly below that skill folder (drag to move · cut and paste — main 2026-11-01 M106 · M110) so that it stops being a skill (P7 · for the CLI · LLM it is `yes` — §1-7). Buttons = delete [Cancel] [Move and remove references] [Move to trash] · `SKILL.md` rename [Cancel] [Rename and remove references] [Rename] · `SKILL.md` move [Cancel] [Move and remove references] [Move] (ADR-0339 · M106). The default detach wording names the three things that drop out (the Engram usage guide · Engram messages · the control channel · turning off claude auto-update — the second was added by T10 · canonical wording = PRD R32) (the old `CodexFilesAsk` was removed — the codex terminal writes nothing to the working folder (P4-FINAL) · the old `RelinkPicker` is replaced by the relink mode of `AddItemsPopover`) | Existing dialog shape (as a whole-window modal) |

**Shortcuts (R50)** — library slot keys go into the existing key table (`src/commands/keybindings.ts` — combination → command, ADR-0055), and **each entry lists the Windows key and the Mac key separately** (the same shape as VS Code's `mac:` and Spacedrive's `{macos, default}`). The OS decision is made in that one table only. No new shortcut library is introduced (swapping Ctrl↔Cmd is one line). Keys are heard only while the slot has focus and are passed on when it is an input field (the existing focus guard `isEditableTarget`). F5 · Ctrl+F · Alt+← → block the webview default behavior (reload · find · history navigation) with `preventDefault` and attach to our behavior. react-arborist's built-in Enter = rename · Backspace = delete collide with the explorer keys, so they are turned off or overridden.

**Data (request type)** — `api/libraryClient.ts` is a thin typed layer over `agentClient.callCommand` (the argument types = the agent crate's generated bindings). `api/libraryQueries.ts` is the **only file** that imports TanStack Query (T1) — key = `[daemonId, 종류, 인자]`. On slot open · focus (slot focus event) · refresh · folder move, that slot's query is invalidated and fetched again. Auto-refetch on window focus and periodic refetch are turned off (R19). An edit reply (save · edit of an agent target) changes the query data at that spot right away. Other slots are brought up to date at their own focus. The tree ⛔ read (§1-7) is also fetched again at the same occasions (tree slot open · focus · refresh) and after the agent list changes (user 2026-10-31 G43 — no new broadcast).

**Edit draft** (user 2026-10-31 G52 「다 수정하고 저장 눌러야되는거 아님? 물론 저장안된건 마킹되있는게 맞고??」 · G53 — reverses memo 41 「슬롯마다 초안 · 저장 단추 없음(바로 적용)」 · memo 83 「+ 담기 바로 적용」 (2026-10-14)) — edits of the preset target `PresetEditor` (add · remove · ⋮⋮ reorder · tag · `AddItemsPopover` check) accumulate only in the draft, and that draft is written to a **separate draft file in the Engram data folder on the shell side** (e.g. `shell\state\drafts\` — not the library folder · not `state.json` · name · shape = client step · key = (daemon identity · library root · library number · preset path) — main 2026-10-31 — M89-REVISED · M92 · the base `rev` is also written as a 「원본이 그 사이 바뀜」 hint — M96 · reading and writing = invoke-only shell commands · outside the shell command table · the content is opaque to the shell · the shape is owned by the frontend — M93 — §1-9 · there is no draft in the daemon or on the bus — G52 · the draft stays even if the tab is moved, attached or detached, or closed or changed by the bus or the LLM · user 2026-10-31 G54 — VS Code hot exit style · replaces M82's shell-memory draft) · while unsaved, a marker is shown (e.g. ● on the tab · title — the shape is client step · the place of the [Save] button is also client step — no picture · M25 · main 2026-10-31 — M77) · an unsaved draft revives with ● when you return to that preset at that number (the draft file stays even if the LLM switched to another preset), reopen library n, restart the app, or after the app dies (on reopening, the preset last shown in the view state and that pair's draft — M89-REVISED · after the app dies, only if the screen restore was accepted · even if declined, the draft is not deleted and appears when that key is opened — M94 · if the daemon · root does not match, it is not attached and stays as an orphan — how it is shown = client step · M92 · if the base `rev` differs from the current one, the 「원본이 그 사이 바뀜」 hint is shown and it is attached anyway — G53 · M96 · user 2026-10-31 G54 — reverses the old 「앱이 죽거나 다시 켜지면 잃는다」 (M77)) · the draft file is deleted on [Save] success · 「저장 안 함」 · [Revert] (save success means only when the unsaved marker is cleared — when the draft is still the sent number r · if it was edited further after sending, the file stays and holds the later edits · main 2026-10-31 — M88 · the save state machine below) · **[Revert]** = discard the draft file, re-read the original and clear the ● (the VS Code 「Revert File」 style · the place (button · menu · shortcut) = client step · user 2026-10-31 G54-ADD) · if the original of a restored draft changed in the meantime, [Save] is last save wins (G53 · details = client step · G54) · [Save] of a restored draft carries the current root generation of `library.status` (the generation is a memory value — M14 · M92) · [Save] = `preset.save` (§1-7 — the draft's four lists · no edition check · last save wins) · **save state machine** (main 2026-10-31 — M81) — the draft has its own number that rises with every edit, and [Save] sends the list of number r · on success, it changes the query data with that reply and clears the unsaved marker only if the draft is still r (an edit made during the save stays unsaved) · on refusal (check M78 · pre-write gate G48 · root generation) it leaves the draft as it is and shows the reason · a close · switch started by 「저장」 proceeds only when the save succeeded and the draft is still the sent number r (not if a relink reply or the like changed the draft in between), otherwise it stays and asks again (main 2026-10-31 — M81-AMEND). When saves overlap, only the reply for the most recently sent number is applied (the replies of earlier numbers are discarded — the shape is client step · main 2026-10-31 — M86). **Checks run on the draft** (main 2026-10-31 — M75 · convention · the user's delegation G53) — the edit area's own checks (conflict lines · the problem list under the header R51 · the popover's 「이미 추가됨」 · cycle disabling) are recounted right away from the draft (like editor diagnostics on an unsaved buffer) · the whole-library state (tree ⛔ · launch blocks · other slots) follows only the saved files — e.g. if a conflicting skill is removed from the draft, the conflict line in the edit area disappears at once, and the tree ⛔ is released after [Save] (the computation = the daemon read `preset.check {path, instructions, skills, settings, presets, folders?}` — the same resolution function as the launch (§1-3 end 「같은 함수가 … 만든다」) · the reply = the same conflict · problem shape as `preset.get` · it does not write · the client does not re-implement the resolution — main 2026-10-31 — M80 · §1-7). **[Relink] · move · rename** (main 2026-10-31 — M76 · M76-REVISED · M76-REVISED-2) — `library.relink` remains the command that applies to the whole library right away without a draft (all presets · agents that point to that old path — R19 · §1-7) · **rewriting draft rows happens only inside the window that issued the command**: the old → new correspondence table of the `library.relink` · `library.move` · `library.rename` reply that the screen of that window received is applied to the unsaved draft rows open in that window (including the draft file — the draft that window opened) (the client's job — so that a later [Save] does not undo the relink · move · M76-REVISED-2) · after the change, of the same path within one list only the first occurrence remains (the same rule as the daemon's rewrite — same path = the same meaning as `same_path` · the shape is client step · main 2026-11-01 M103 · ADR-0339) · for the reply of a delete without references · `SKILL.md` rename · move out (the removed paths — §1-7 「공통 인자 · 답 꼴」), the row of that path is removed from the draft open in that window (the other edits of the draft stay — so that a later [Save] does not revive the removed row · M103 · ADR-0339) · a delete reply received by the same window deletes that draft file (main 2026-10-31 — M96), and a rename · move reply also **moves the open draft's key · save target to the new path** (and deletes the old draft file) and the view-state selection follows the same correspondence table (main 2026-10-31 — M91) · ghost prevention covers only a delete in the same window — if the LLM · another window · the outside deletes it and recreates it at the same path, it is attached with the hint (G53 · the general guarantee = a stable identity per file — G38 · T-74 + the ADR-0325 amendment · not now · M96) · the sweep target = the drafts of presets that were deleted, renamed or moved (shape = client step · M91) · the drafts of other windows · draft files not open in any window (main 2026-10-31 — M88 · looked at again in the client step — NF3) · a relink · move · other change made by the LLM does not reach them (there is no broadcast — 「감시 · 방송 없음」 as it is · no new broadcast is created) — if such a draft is saved later, the old path is written again and that row becomes 「찾을 수 없음」 again (fix it with relink · G53 last save wins — accepted). If that preset itself was moved, renamed or deleted in the meantime, [Save] is refused with `NOT_FOUND` and is not recreated at the old path (§1-7 `preset.save` · main 2026-10-31 — M76-REVISED-2). If the same preset is opened in two slots, each has its own draft and the last save wins (the drafts of other slots are not merged — G53 · the draft file is also per number · preset pair, so there are two — `Library{n}` is one slot per number · VS Code's shared buffer needs real-time sync between webviews, so simplicity was chosen · main 2026-10-31 — M89 · M89-REVISED). **The three below = main 2026-10-31 — convention · the user's delegation G53** (「관행보고 따라」): ① **leaving without saving** (user 2026-10-31 G54 · main 2026-10-31 — M87 · M90 · M95) — 「변경 내용을 저장할까요?」 [Save] [Don't save] [Cancel] (Notepad · VS Code · Office style · 「저장」 = proceed only after it succeeds — M81 · 「저장 안 함」 = delete the draft file and proceed · 「취소」 = stay) is raised **by the webview holding the draft, inside the human-gesture handler, before it calls the command** · the gestures that prompt are only these (exhaustive): tab ✕ · slot close · preset switch (picking in the center area) · the ✕ of the app title bar of a pop-out window (closing the unsaved library tabs of that window — M87 narrowed to this button · VS Code asks when a window that is not the last is closed) · a shell command · registry · bus command · OS `CloseRequested` do not prompt — Alt+F4 on a pop-out and closing from the taskbar also do not prompt and the draft file stays (the same as an LLM close · the premise that closing a pop-out cannot be cancelled stays — S21-slot-docking Q12 · `/qa` prevent_close check · M90) · other content changes also deliberately do not prompt and keep the draft — slot menu 「새 콘텐츠」 · drag-drop → `layout.setSlotContent` · `slot.assignAgent` · `libraryView.reveal` · folder move · choosing a non-preset (M95) · closing the whole dashboard (tray 「완전 종료」 — the main ✕ hides, so it does not close the tab and does not prompt · ADR-0026 · ADR-0229) does not prompt (the draft file stays and shows ● at the next start — VS Code hot exit style · G54 · M95) · moving a tab · closing or changing the selection by the bus · LLM neither prompts nor blocks (the draft file stays — G54 · removes M82's `CONFLICT` · `yes` · §1-7) ② **an agent target has no draft** — attaching · detaching · ordering presets in the agent slot · block window are called right away as now (the properties-panel convention — the Unreal Details panel · the VS Code settings UI apply immediately · the draft rule belongs to the preset document itself) ③ **no notice banner is shown that the disk changed while a draft is open** (the Notepad rule · the user wants it simple) — saving overwrites. The LLM produces the same result with `preset.save` (the same handle) · `preset.*` (CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」) · **known gap** — the LLM cannot see, save or discard a person's edit draft (G52 · M93 · §1-7). **Client step notes** (main 2026-10-31 — re-review r11 N1 · N5 · N6 · r12 R2 · R3 · R4 · not changed now): the race between an immediate write and unmount · tab move (the guarantee reaches the last write — N1) · a lost save reply → ● on a draft identical to the file · a draft hidden under another number · endless accumulation (N5) · the draft file does an atomic write by itself with `base::file` · outside the shell state sweep · the file name is owned by the draft store (ADR-0264 — N6) · a rename by another window · the LLM detaches the open draft → [Save] `NOT_FOUND` (look at save-as · running the name correspondence table over all of the shell's own windows — R2) · the prompt gate wraps menus · gesture handlers and does not wrap registry commands · future key bindings · palette either pass through this gate or do not prompt · several unsaved tabs on a pop-out app title bar ✕ (R3) · re-keying to an existing draft key · the order of re-keying and picking · choosing the root string inside the key (R4).

**Display** — a missing item appears in the edit area · agent slot as a dotted row 「찾을 수 없음 · [다시 연결] ×」 (there is no candidate-suggestion row — B11 · [Relink] from the 1st edition — P1), and the preset row that contains that row gets a red error-count badge, and one line also goes into the problem list under the header (`PresetEditor` row · B3). The old 「다시 연결됨 · 이전 경로」 · 「다시 연결할까요? · [확인]」 marker (Δd) was removed. The agent slot header = name + backend · mode (the agent value · fixed at creation · R31 — the working folder is seen and changed not in the header but in the 「작업 폴더」 area) — the old 「기본 제공 백엔드의 흐린 「사용할 수 없음」 row」 and the codex file reply were removed (user 2026-10-18). If the launch success reply has the notice list `notices`, the screen shows it as a notice popup (non-blocking · wording = the code + arguments the daemon emitted · P4-FINAL · G28 — the shape is client step). The favorites header and collection component in the left area are not in this edition (G34 — deferred).

**Folder section (ADR-0341 · review round 1)** — `PresetEditor`'s folder section adds to the **draft**, and `preset.save` does not re-check existence (§1-7 — a vanished folder is legal content), so the typo guard sits in front of the draft: when a path is typed or comes back from 「찾아보기」, the section first calls `preset.previewFolder {folder}` (§1-7 — the read already defined; its answer says whether `preset.add` would accept the path) and refuses a path that is not an existing, readable folder (missing · access denied · stat timeout), a relative path or a cmd-unsafe path with the same code + args as a `preset.add` refusal — the path does not enter the draft. A folder that disappears after it entered the draft or the file stays and is saved as is (its line shows 「폴더를 찾을 수 없음」). Paths that pass enter the draft in the normalized form the answer returns (§1-2), and a path equal to one already in the draft after normalization is skipped, like `preset.add`. The same answer fills the line's detail card (scan preview), so one read serves both.

**Block window** (G13 · user 2026-10-20 「저거 띄우면 되는거 아니냐」 · 「단독 슬롯으로되야함. 왜냐하면 창 크기에 막히면 안되니깐. 그냥 독단적으로 툴팁같이 뜨면됨」 — replaces the old 「막힘 팝업」 (a non-modal card beside the agent with a reason for each blocked item · item link → library · 2026-10-18) · closing rule = G13 addendum — research = `docs/research/launch-blocked-popup-navigation-practice-2026-10-20.md` · convention = a panel that opens by itself and stays (Unreal Compiler Results) · it stays non-modal — `docs/research/blocked-launch-error-display-practice-2026-10-18.md`)
1. When a **launch made by a person** is blocked (double-click · menu — activation is one request, and when its error reply carries a structured block reason · G15 · §1-8), the shell raises that agent's block window (`LaunchBlockedWindow`) — a separate floating OS window · beside that row in the agent tree · inside = that agent's `PresetEditor` (the problem list above = the same lines as the four block reasons · including the one agent-level item (library folder missing) — B3). If that agent's window already exists, it is reused (main-session decision — reported).
2. **Fixing happens inside that window** — pressing a problem row = scroll · focus · flash at that spot (G5) · × = remove (saved immediately) · [Relink] search popover (R19 · 1st edition — P1). Close = × · Esc only (it does not close on outside press or on losing focus — G13 addendum · user 「ㅇㅋㅇㅋ … 최종 적용」 · edits are saved immediately, so nothing is lost).
3. **Going to the library happens only when the user goes to the file** — double-click on that row · magnifier (R28 · the current path as is) = the shell command `libraryView.reveal {path}` (number omitted = the Δ6 rule: the last touched one if a library 1~4 is open · if none, 「라이브러리 1」 is opened — it opens through the same path as the menu above (a floating window), but that path belongs to the docking work (R42 · wt2) and is not on this branch, and until then the open-position rule of `slot.openUnique` applies). If the first scan has not happened yet, it selects after a brief loading (flow ①). The right-hand detail area shows the reason · row · location (R15 error detail). A missing file cannot be selected, so select the **preset that references** that item. The block window stays as it is (because it does not close on outside press).
4. Agent preset slot — the same component, so the same problem list (press = G5) · a red error-count badge on the preset row that has block items (while collapsed · including what comes up from inside — `PresetEditor` row) · expanding shows ⛔ + the reason on that row · double-click · magnifier = the same as 3. This slot marker and the ⛔ of the agent tree (blocked if launched now — computed on the spot · P8) remain after the block window is closed (whether to also leave the blocked launch on the old failure-marker path = open item §2-4).
5. A launch made by the LLM · CLI — the block window is not raised · the tree ⛔ + the same content is received as the code + arguments of the `agent.spawn` error reply (R49).
- There is no new global handle · webview registry command — it stands on top of the existing shell commands (`libraryView.reveal` · `slot.openUnique`) and the block window the shell creates (§1-9 — the command that raises it is added to the existing `agentPresets.*` group · name · shape left to implementation) (CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」).

**View state** — the authority is the shell (§1-9 · the edit draft is separate — the draft file on the shell side · §1-10 「편집 초안」 · G54). `store/libraryViewStore.ts` (zustand) is a **drawing copy** that holds the `library-view:updated` the shell emitted, and a gesture invokes `libraryView.*`. Only momentary values such as the area width while dragging stay in the webview, and the finished value is sent with `libraryView.setLayout`.

**Menus · entry**
- The empty slot 「새 콘텐츠 ▸ 라이브러리 ▸ 1~4」 — one more level of submenu (ADR-0065 addendum · §3) · the 「프리셋 팔레트」 item is removed.
- Agent tree row menu 「프리셋…」 → `agentPresets.open` (an inline menu, but it is called by command id).
- Agent tree row menu 「복제」 → `agent.duplicate {agent}` (B13 · 1st edition · the LLM calls the same command — §1-5 「복제」).
- The agent tree panel menu 「에이전트 생성 ▸ claude/codex × JSON/터미널」 is left as it is, and at creation it asks for the folder as now (the existing picker window — later changed in the 「작업 폴더」 area · creation without a path = next topic §8) — the daemon attaches only the default preset, and the backend · mode chosen in the menu are fixed as agent values (user 2026-10-18 — the old 「기본 + 맞는 기본 제공 백엔드 프리셋(Δ1)」). Activation is one request — if the error reply is a block, the block window (above — no pre-check · there is no asking dialog · G15 · G13).
- Folder magnifier (Δ6) — in an agent slot, the last touched library slot, and if none, the smallest of the closed numbers · in a library slot area title, **another** library slot (the same rule, excluding itself) · sets `필터 ▾` to that kind (D7).

### 1-11. Scenarios

> **The details of the client step are settled after the server step** (G20 — direction = G19 · G22 · G23 · the head of §1-9). The screen flows below are a draft — the server step verifies the same commands with the `engram` CLI (§1-7).

| Situation | Flow |
|---|---|
| First launch | If no root has ever been written at the daemon's first start, it creates an empty default root (`<배포판>\library` — development · test = the `DataLayout` rule) and writes it as the root (G35 · §1-4 「루트 · 기본 경로」) → 「라이브러리 ▸ 1」 → `library.status` = ok · empty library notice (R8). If it could not be created, it stays as `unset` (M24-2) with an in-empty-tree notice + [Open folder] (shell `os_pick_folder` → `library.setRoot`) · [Create new] (a picker window filled with the suggested path → `library.setRoot {create:true}`) |
| Folder vanished | `library.status` = missing → the same place 「<경로>를 찾을 수 없습니다」 + the same buttons · the values stay as they are (R8) |
| Pop-out | Slot pop-out as is · the number state · selection are in the shell, so ⇐ works even across different windows |
| Reconnect · daemon restart | Query fails → after the connection recovers, fetch again on focus · refresh. `rev` is a stable content hash (M21), so it is the same value after a restart — if the file is unchanged, the first reorder also works as is (removes the old 「재시작 뒤 첫 순서 바꾸기는 헛 `CONFLICT`」) |
| Old data | Discarded without asking (G32) — the new edition uses a new profile file name, so it starts with an empty registry and neither reads nor deletes the old `agents.json` (no migration · no backup — §1-5 「옛 에이전트」 · D4 · M25) · `presets.json` is the same (ADR-0264 「옛 데이터는 버린다」) · old preset palette slot = empty slot |

### 1-11a. Flow check — server hops (G17)

> 2026-10-31 · CLI ⇄ daemon (control route · command bus) ⇄ asset-library (design) ⇄ agent (manager · backend). Code pointers = HEAD `5b6bb6e1` (what the review pointed at was corrected to the lines after the merge `2ced4784` — review 2026-10-31). Notation: ⇄ = a round trip across the process boundary (HTTP · WS) · → = a synchronous call inside the same process · [ ] = what is held at that hop — L-read · L-write = the asset-library `RwLock` · R = the `ProfileRegistry` lock · S = the manager reservation table (`spawning`) · Ss = the manager session table. Round trip = one request · reply pair crossing the boundary. Fixed spots are marked 「흐름 점검 G17」 in each section.

**Common entrances (measured)**
- CLI — `engram <이름> --인자` ⇄ `POST /control/call` → `spawn_blocking` → `catalog::handle_call` → `call_daemon_command(caller=None)` (`control/commands.rs:170` — the only entrance for the arguments · input-lease check) → table handler (`blocking_handler`). Refusal body = `{status:"error", code, hint}` (`control/ingress.rs:442`) — the CLI prints the body it receives as is. Array arguments stand as JSON text (`--paths '["a"]'` — the schema decides the type).
- Shell — webview → shell → WS envelope ⇄ → `command_delivery::deliver` → `locals.claim` (the daemon's own name) → `spawn_local` (blocking pool) → `call_daemon_command(caller=Some(연결))`. Only a launch made by a person goes wire `SpawnProfile` → `spawn_blocking` → `activate_profile` (`connection_core.rs:1641`).
- Deadline — bus 7 seconds (`command_delivery.rs:460`) · CLI silence 10 seconds (`agent/src/types.rs:521`). Past it, the caller gets a deadline error and the body runs to the end (the blocking pool has no cancellation). If sent again — bus = a mutating command with the same request number is `ALREADY_APPLIED` (`retains_the_id` — only `INVALID_ARGUMENT` · `NOT_FOUND` · `CONFLICT` release the number) · the CLI's daemon's own name = it does not pass through the slot table and is applied again (`control/catalog.rs:147-161`).

**Scenarios**

1. **claude + preset launch** (CLI · 2 round trips — 3 if attaching is done separately)
   - `agent.new --backend claude --cwd D --presets '["P"]'` ⇄ → `verb_new` → attach check (`LibraryCommandHost` — the profile to be built · outside the registry)[L-read] → manager `create_agent`[R] → profile edit port → manager[R] → reply `{agentId}` + the skipped presets (partial apply — only the ones that do not fit are left out and it is built · finding 7 · user 2026-10-31 G41 · the check comes before the build — review 2026-10-31).
   - `agent.spawn --target <id>` ⇄ → `wake_existing` (resolution · registry copy · `can_resume_profile`) → `activate_profile` → reservation[S — taken and released] → running?[Ss] → `ensure_registered`[R] → preprocessing: re-read the registry[R released] → `kits.check` (write down the mutation · root generation · swap-in counts[scan generation `Mutex` briefly — no waiting] → re-check the attached files on disk[no lock · the number read before opening, per file] → `resolve` · `validate`[L-read — released and left] → if new bytes, merge `Catalog`[L-write briefly — if the generation moved in between, discard the whole thing · if only a swap-in intervened, discard only the insertion of paths not in `Catalog` · the rest is kept by whichever read later] — never take L-write while holding L-read · it waits for neither a scan nor a ticket · flow ① 「잠금」 ③ · ④ · review 2026-10-31 · R2 · R3) → compare-and-set[R — even when blocked · including command · env] → update `register_for_spawn`[R] (none if the id was just inserted) → `epoch_for_spawn`[R] → re-read the registry[R] (after the marker — finding 19 · §1-5 (가) 3) → canonicalize → `provision` (control channel) → `kits.prepare` (the merged instructions · merged settings in the volatile folder)[L-read] → `materialize` (`LaunchFs` — `--plugin-dir` junction) → `build_command_spec` · `open_spawn` → session registration → pump → reply `{started, notices}` (finding 1) → registry notification.
   - A screen launch is the same from `activate_profile` in the second line (one wire `SpawnProfile` · reply `Spawned` / `AgentEvent::Error`).
2. **codex JSON** (1–2 round trips) — the same up to the `materialize` of 1 (`extraRoots` junction) → `open_spawn` → waits until the link connection finishes (cap 15 seconds — longer than the bus deadline of 7 seconds · the same today) → `skills/extraRoots/set` → new conversation = `thread/start.developerInstructions` · resume = `additionalContext` on the first turn only when the last-applied hash[R read] differs from the new hash → in the first-turn echo, persist the session id (latch) and the hash (finding 12 · the hash is a mutate[R] separate from the compare-and-set — it is written even when the compare-and-set does not write on a resume · review 2026-10-31) → reply.
3. **codex terminal** (1 round trip) — `kits.check` puts the instructions · skills that get dropped into `notices` (what to put = the backend port — finding 13) → `provision` → `kits.prepare` (「기본」 priming only) → no `materialize` → `build_command_spec` (`-c developer_instructions=` + the preset `-c` line · length = finding 10) → `open_spawn` → reply `notices` → the CLI prints it · nothing goes to the launched agent (G28).
4. **Block** (1 round trip · spawn · no control channel issued) — all four arise inside `kits.check` (the lock = that line of 1 · review 2026-10-31): ① not found = not in `Catalog` and not found in the disk re-check either ② error = YAML · cycle · ~~schema type/range~~ (dropped — ADR-0359) · codex JSON skill format · `-c` guard · length (the cap count — only the step 7 re-check is after `provision`, so a guard cleans up after it · finding 10) ③ conflict = `resolve` ④ library folder = root state. Reply — CLI = refusal body (code `CONFLICT` · the four reasons in the argument field · exit 1 — findings 2 · 3) · bus = `CommandReply` error (argument field) · wire = widened `AgentEvent::Error`. The creating verb (`agent.spawn --cwd`) is as above if blocked in the pre-create check, and if blocked after creating, code `INTERNAL` + the created agent id · reason in the argument field (§1-5 (가) 1 ② · review 2026-10-31). (Step 16 as landed: the CLI refusal body is `{status, code, hint, args?}` with no roster tail on a launch refusal. Step 16 deferred the pre-create check; step 20 (`0badd007`) landed it, so a `--cwd` block before the add answers `CONFLICT` + `reasons` with nothing created and only a block after the add answers `INTERNAL` + `args.agent_id` + `reasons` — §5-2 20.) `notices` is not carried in a block reply (only in the success reply · `agent.presets`). Two compare-and-set failures are not a block reason (`CONFLICT` 「다시 시도」).
5. **Rename · interrupted midway** (1 round trip · if there is a referenced `SKILL.md` then `yes` ⇒ 2 · if `detachRefs` then, the 「프리셋 파일 하나씩」 below is removing that skill folder's line instead of a rewrite, and the 「에이전트 붙임」 step does not exist — an agent attaches only presets · ADR-0339) — `library.rename` ⇄ → entrance: if before the first scan, start it or wait[no lock · no ticket yet and no in-progress count either — if reversed, it blocks itself · R2] → scan-generation ticket (enters once the earlier tickets have all gone in and the in-progress scans have finished · a scan that came after cannot jump ahead · R2) → count as a mutation in progress (meanwhile no new scan starts · flow ① 「잠금」 ② · review 2026-10-31) → [L-write throughout] plan (`refs.rs` — `Catalog` + registry read[R] · the registry read belongs to the `yes` decision) → `FsStore::rename` → atomic write of the preset files one by one → agent attach = the profile edit port (correspondence table only) → manager[R — holds it once and rewrites the whole current registry · lines up with duplicate · review 2026-10-31] → fix `Catalog` → raise the change generation[inside L-write · before release — a launch check that read before that and arrived late, and the merge of the list safety net, see this and discard · R2] → reply. (a) **Only the caller is cut off** (deadline · CLI exit) — the body runs to the end and only the reply is lost. If sent again, bus = `ALREADY_APPLIED` · CLI = applied again → no old path → item result `NOT_FOUND`. Confirm with `library.read` · `library.list`. (b) **Daemon death** (G38) — the side that finished remains, and the line that could not be fixed = not found → after restart, one `library.relink {from, to}` ⇄ fixes the remaining presets · agents. Leftover temp files are not cleaned up (the scan skips them and the next write does not overwrite them — finding 15 · user 2026-10-31 G40 · main 2026-10-31 M56).
6. **Delete · restore** — `library.delete` ⇄ → (join of the entrance's first scan · ticket · in-progress count · raising the change generation before release = flow ① 「잠금」 ② — the same as 5) → [L-write] collect references (for a folder, everything inside · `Catalog` + registry[R]) → references present · `yes` absent = `CONFLICT` (the items involved · references · two shapes `--yes` / `--yes --detach-refs` — name placeholder · ADR-0339) ⇒ again with one of them (2 round trips) → [inside L-write · raise the waiting count → trash `Mutex`] `.trash\<묶음>\<원래 경로>` rename (per item · one operation = one bundle · the count is lowered after the last move — review 2026-10-31 R6) → remove from `Catalog` → (if also removing references) fix references = the same path as 5 — remove the line from the preset files one by one (atomic write · presets that went to `.trash` in the same operation are skipped — M105) → agent attach = the profile edit port `remove_preset_paths(paths)` (name placeholder — paths = the preset files among the deleted paths · for a folder, down to the presets among its descendants · counted when it runs — M105 · an agent attaches only presets · if there is no such path or it is a `SKILL.md` delete, this step does not exist · holds the whole registry once · lines up with duplicate · main 2026-11-01 M101)[R] → fix `Catalog` (presets whose line was removed — the same as 5) → per-item result + removed paths (ADR-0339). If keeping, the references are not fixed → from that moment they are not found (live comparison) · the skill junction of a running agent is cut (finding 14 — the same behavior as the CLI skill folder today · no separate handling · user 2026-10-31 G42). Restore = `library.trash` ⇄ (walks `.trash` on the disk — outside `Catalog`) → `library.restore` ⇄ → (ticket · in-progress count · raise the change generation = the same ② · whether to join the first scan = the §1-7 「첫 훑기」 list) → [L-write → raise the waiting count → trash `Mutex`] rename each item to its original path (creating the parent folder) → read that file and put it into `Catalog` (with several items, the count is lowered after the last move · review 2026-10-31 R6) → the same name = a conflict for that item ⇒ again by choosing another name (round trip +1).
7. **Reconnect** — the CLI has no state (one HTTP per command). If the shell WS is cut, the replies to in-flight requests sent over that connection are lost and the daemon body runs to the end. The library has no per-connection state · subscription (request type), so there is nothing to realign — on focus · refresh the screen does `library.status` (root generation · `daemon_id`) ⇄ + one ⇄ per visible slot. The view state is shell authority, so it stays as is · `rev` is a stable hash. On a daemon restart, `Catalog` is empty and the first request waits for the first scan (deadline — finding 17). The agent list is the resync path as it is today.
8. ~~**New schema version**~~ — **dropped by ADR-0359** (no schema to follow). Was: after the daemon assembly, one daemon thread (finding 4) → asset-library schema update (outside the lock) → backend port version read (`--version` child process) → if it differs from the cached version, the fetch port (the daemon's HTTPS — 1 outside round trip) → write `daemon\cache\schemas\` → plug the new schema into the checker → scan-generation ticket (as a mutation — there is no join of the entrance's first scan · it does not trigger the first scan · if there is a scan walking or queued ahead, FIFO puts it behind that) → count as a mutation in progress → [L-write briefly] re-check all settings (the `Catalog` items and the check results of the parse cache · before the first scan the `Catalog` is empty, so there is nothing to do — the later first scan runs with the new schema) → raise the change generation[inside L-write · before release — the late merge of a launch check that checked with the old schema sees this and discards] → ⛔ is recomputed by the next query (flow ① 「잠금」 ② · ④ · review 2026-10-31 R3). The first scan that was walking when the new schema arrived publishes results checked with the old schema, and this re-check that follows fixes them — a read answered in that gap (such as a list attached to that first scan) may be an old-schema result. Failure = keep what it has. The trigger is the daemon start, once — if the CLI version changes while running, it is not known until the next start (reads the 「판이 바뀌면」 of §1-4 `validate.rs` as a comparison at start). The wire version (`PROTOCOL_VERSION` 8 · catalog 9) is such that the handshake refuses an old shell and the CLI reads the daemon table, so it is unaffected.
9. **First-start default root** (G35 · 0 round trips) — daemon boot → `DataLayout` (with the root's origin) → default root place → `LibraryService` assembly (before the accept loop · no lock — finding 4): no `library.json` → create the folder → `library.json` atomic write → `ok` · on failure, it is not written and `unset` (since it is not written, the next start tries again — read). The first scan does not run until the first request. The later `library.status` ⇄ = `ok` / `unset`.
10. **Empty the trash** (shell-only · 1 round trip) — confirmation modal → webview → shell → WS ⇄ → `deliver` → `spawn_local` → `call_daemon_command(caller=Some)` → passes the shell-only check → [library lock · ticket · no join of the first scan: `.trash` is outside `Catalog`, so it is outside flow ① 「잠금」 ② · §3 · review 2026-10-31 R3] [L-read briefly] reads the root once → after releasing [if the waiting count is greater than 0, waits without a lock until it becomes 0 (for the moves of one operation to finish — R6) → trash `Mutex` briefly] takes a copy of the `.trash` bundle list once (empty-all takes only those still present among the bundle ids the screen sent — main 2026-10-31 — M83 · if given `items`, it groups those items by bundle · review 2026-10-31 R4 · a move holds the trash `Mutex`, and taking the list is after that wait, so it does not capture a half-filled bundle — R5 · R6) → for each bundle [if the waiting count is greater than 0, waits without a lock until it becomes 0 (handover — R5) → takes the trash `Mutex` → that bundle (empty-all = the whole bundle folder — user 2026-10-31 G44 · `items` = the given items of that bundle — R5) to the platform OS trash (`IFileOperation` directly — M57 · each call waits on a new STA thread with a join — M64 · each item has its own `PerformOperations` — M66 · handed over and the OS decides — user 2026-10-31 G47 · a portable result per item — M62: nothing after the call = emptied · refused by the OS and remaining = not deleted and left in `.trash` · a path that is already absent before the call = `NOT_FOUND` (M71 · M85) · if sending the whole bundle folder fails, the items remaining in that bundle are retried one by one — since the result is per item, one open or locked file remains in `.trash` and only its own item is caught · review 2026-10-31 R6) → release] → per-item result (empty-all per bundle — for a bundle re-sent per item, that result carries the per-item results · R6 · an item left in `.trash` carries its reason `{code, args}` (the original code is in the diagnostic field) — G47 · M62). A bundle created after taking the copy is not touched · an item restored and gone before its turn is, in empty-all, absent from that bundle folder and so does not go along, and in `items` that item's result is `NOT_FOUND` · even if the root changes after taking the copy, the emptying continues with the paths of the root it took (it does not move to the new root), and if that path has vanished, that bundle · item is `NOT_FOUND`. CLI · agent = `/control/call` ⇄ → `call_daemon_command(caller=None)` → the check refuses — it does not reach the table handler (finding 16). A large trash may exceed the deadline of 7 seconds (the body runs to the end) — emptying itself does not stall launch · list · edit. A delete · overwriting move · restore that comes in during the emptying raises the waiting count and waits for the trash `Mutex` while holding the write lock, but the emptying releases per bundle and does not take the next bundle until that count becomes 0 (handover — it does not depend on the re-acquire race of the unfair `Mutex` · R5), so that wait is at most the time of sending the current one bundle (the emptying takes the next bundle only after the one that came in behind has finished all of its own moves — R6) and only during that time does the read lock of launch · list also stand (review 2026-10-31 R4 — if even one bundle is long = finding 17).

**Where the same command crosses the boundary twice** — there is none on the launch path (the old `agent.launchCheck` was removed · the block window's `agent.presets` is material for the edit area, so it is not a repeat of the same command). The one that remained = the `agent.presets` re-request after an agent preset edit (finding 8).

**Findings**
1. 「고침」 There was no path to carry blocks · notices from the manager to the two entrances — `AgentCommandHost::activate_profile` is `Result<StartedAgent, PtyError>` (`commands.rs:493`) · `wake_existing` · `create_and_start` folds every failure into `INTERNAL` (`commands.rs:1156` · `:1196`) · the wire has string errors (`profile_error_text` — `connection_core.rs:1785-1794`) · `Spawned` carries only `AgentInfo` → §1-5 (가) 1.
2. 「고침」 The block reply had no outer error code — if `INTERNAL`, `retains_the_id` (`command_delivery.rs:1440`) holds on to the number, and a launch sent again with the same number after fixing is swallowed as `ALREADY_APPLIED` → outer = `CONFLICT` · a compare-and-set failure is also `CONFLICT` (§1-5 (가) 1 · 2). But for the verbs that create an agent (`agent.spawn --cwd` · `agent.new {presets}`), only a block in the pre-create check is `CONFLICT` · a block that arises after creation is `INTERNAL`, which holds on to the number, + the created agent id (if the number were released, a retry would create a duplicate — ADR-0122 · §1-5 (가) 1 ② · review 2026-10-31).
3. 「고침」 The CLI refusal body drops the argument field — `catalog::refused` (`control/catalog.rs:392`) folds `CommandError` only into `{code, hint}` · this document widened only the bus envelope and the wire → §1-8 (the CLI code stays as is).
4. 「고침」 Assembly cycle — the manager holds `LaunchKitSource` (= `LibraryService`) and `LibraryService` holds the profile edit port (= the manager) · who stands up first was undefined · the owner and timing of the schema update thread · the first-start default root · the `launch\` sweep were also undefined → §1-4 「조립 순서」.
5. 「고침」 `agent.presets` Among the lock reasons, asset-library had no way to know 「실행 중」 — running is in the manager session table (`manager.rs:906`) and not in the registry → a lock read on the profile edit port (§1-5).
6. 「고침」 If asset-library resolves the agent designation by itself, it becomes a different rule from `agent.spawn` → the table assembly part resolves it with the same interpreter and passes the id (§1-5 「명령 어휘」).
7. 「고침(배선) · **닫힘**(정책 — 사용자 2026-10-31 G41)」 `agent.new {presets?}` had no owner of the validation (`verb_new` calls only the manager — `commands.rs:1257`) → the `attachPreset` body is inside the same handler (§1-7) · the validation is done on the profile to be built **before** `create_agent` · a failure after creation is `INTERNAL` + the created agent id (§1-5 (가) 1 ② — ADR-0122 duplicate prevention · review 2026-10-31). Policy = **partial apply** (Δ7 — the same rule as attaching · user 「ㅇㅇ」): if one preset does not fit, only that one is skipped, the agent is built with only the fitting ones attached, and the skipped ones are carried in the reply (§1-7 `agent.new` row · scenario 1) — the old branch 「통째로 거절 — 아무것도 안 만들고 `CONFLICT`」 is removed.
8. 「고침」 The `agent.attachPreset` · `detachPreset` · `reorderPresets` replies were only a one-line result, so the screen called `agent.presets` again after every edit (`preset.*` carries the new state) → the new state is in the reply (§1-7).
9. 「고침」 Scan and the single `RwLock` — if the write lock is held while walking, the launch check waits for the whole scan (「실행은 기다리지 않는다」 · M11 · 「훑기 동안 편집은 기다린다」 — the three did not hold together) → flow ① 「잠금」. Review 2026-10-31 fixed more — the launch check blocked itself trying to fix `Catalog` while holding the read lock · a folder scan reverted a launch update outside its range · a scan started during a rename carried the old path again → the same 「잠금」 ② – ④. Review 2026-10-31 R2 fixed more — the late merge of a launch check · list safety net that came in after a finished rename · delete · preset edit revived the old path · old bytes (the change generation blocked only scans) · newly arriving scans jumped ahead of a waiting mutation endlessly (no fairness) · if an edit counted as in progress and then waited for the first scan, it blocked itself → the same 「잠금」 ② · ④ · ⑤. Review 2026-10-31 R3 fixed more — the launch merge, which does not move the generation after the swap-in of a scan that overwrote a P deleted from outside, revived P · a `library.refresh` attached to a scan that was already walking returned bytes that missed an outside change before the call (PRD R41) · the settings re-check for the new schema wrote outside the ticket · generation, so the first scan that was walking published an old-schema result → the same 「잠금」 ② · ④ · ⑤. Review 2026-10-31 R4 fixed more — if the first library call after restarting the daemon was a folder `library.refresh`, it scanned only that folder, raised a partial `Catalog`, and answered references outside the folder as false 「찾을 수 없음」 → the same 「잠금」 ⑤ (a refresh before the first scan goes through the first-scan entrance · 「첫 훑기 끝」 = the full scan has been raised). Review 2026-10-31 R5 fixed more — if the first scan that a folder refresh before the first scan was waiting for failed and was not raised, the awakened refresh scanned only that folder with a ticket and raised a partial `Catalog` → the same 「잠금」 ⑤ (on waking, it looks at 「첫 훑기 끝」 again, and if not, it goes through the first-scan entrance again).
10. 「고침」 When the command-line length is decided — the final argv stands only at step 7 · today the codex terminal priming drops silently if it exceeds the budget (`backend/codex/mod.rs:369`) → §1-5 「명령줄 가드」 (cap count + step 7 re-check · priming included).
11. 「고침」 If `kits.release` takes the library lock, the reaper stands behind a long write lock · the boot sweep's junction rule · being called twice was undefined → the §1-4 `launch.rs` row.
12. 「고침」 It was undefined when to write the codex JSON last-applied instructions hash — if written at spawn, a resume after an incarnation whose first turn never went out skips the changed instructions → at the time the first turn reached the other side (the latch spot · §1-5 profile table). But it is not put on the session-id compare-and-set — the compare-and-set of a resume has the same value and so does not write, and the hash does not change → a separate incarnation-marker guard mutate that runs on the latch's first-turn event (review 2026-10-31).
13. 「고침」 The resolution input had no mode, and the place of 「codex 터미널은 지침 · 스킬을 싣지 않는다」 knowledge was `resolve.rs` (a 「백엔드 확장」 violation) → mode in the §1-3 input · 「싣는 것」 on the backend settings check port.
14. 「**닫힘 — 사용자 2026-10-31 G42**」 The skills of a running agent point at the library folder live through a junction — if that skill folder is moved, renamed or deleted, the skill disappears from that session (junction cut) or an operation fails with 「열려 있음」 · an edit of a skill file is seen at once (instructions · settings are copies at launch, so from the next launch). Accepted — the same behavior as the CLI skill folder today, so no separate handling (user 「그건 기존에도 똑같은거 아냐?」) · one line in the §6-3 risks.
15. 「**닫힘 — 사용자 2026-10-31 G40**」 (old 「메인 확인 — 메모와 걸림」) The temp file 「부팅 쓸기」 (old §1-4 `store.rs` · flow ④ · memo G38 「swept at boot」) would have to walk the whole root and collided with 「데몬이 켜질 때 미리 훑지 않는다」 (user 2026-10-17) → the sweep was removed (user 「남기고 알아서 치우라그래」): temp file = a `base::file::write_atomic` temp `<이름>.tmp<pid>.<번호>` in the same folder (main 2026-10-31 M56 — replaces the old 「파일마다 고정 이름 `<파일>.engram-tmp`」 · no second rule is created) · not deleted automatically (neither at boot nor on scan · `base::file::sweep_temps` is not called) · the scan skips that name shape (it does not enter the list · problem list) · the next write does not overwrite it (pid · number in the name — accepted · M56) (§1-2 kind decision · §1-4 `store.rs` · flow ④). The two old recommendations (delete it as the first scan passes · a hidden fixed place inside the root) were removed. Research = `docs/research/atomic-write-temp-cleanup-practice-2026-10-31.md` (the same shape as VS Code `.vsctmp`).
16. 「고침」 There was no place for the shell-only refusal — a table handler cannot see the calling connection (handler `call(args)` — `command/src/table.rs:16`) → a name check in `call_daemon_command`'s `caller` (the §1-7 `library.emptyTrash` row).
17. 「확인 항목」 Waiting work (the `library.list` that waits for the first scan — after review 2026-10-31, `preset.get` · `agent.presets` and every other command that reads `Catalog` · a folder `library.refresh` before the first scan also waits for the whole first scan — R4 · a big move · relink · emptying the trash (outside the library lock — the trash `Mutex` is taken and released per bundle, and if there is a waiting side it is handed over before the next bundle (it does not depend on the re-acquire race of the unfair `Mutex` — R5), so a delete · restore that came in during the emptying and waits while holding the write lock — during which the read lock of launch · list also stands — lasts at most the time of sending one bundle · R3 · R4 · R5) · an edit waiting for the scan of an earlier ticket — R2) exceeding the bus 7 seconds · CLI 10 seconds means the caller gets a deadline error and the body runs to the end — if sent again, the first scan waits for the same scan by merging. The yardstick of the first-scan measurement (§6-2) = 7 seconds. If even sending one bundle (a bundle from deleting a very large folder) is too slow, the next step is staging — inside the trash `Mutex`, rename the bundle that was copied aside to a hidden folder inside `.trash` and send it without a lock (only when the measurement calls for it · review 2026-10-31 R4).
18. 「**닫힘 — 사용자 2026-10-31 G43**」 There is no path to carry the tree ⛔ — it is not at the place §1-3 「싣는 꼴 = §1-7」 points to. There is no broadcast, so after the LLM · CLI changes the library, the tree ⛔ stays stale until the next query. → one bus read (the §1-7 「트리 ⛔ 읽기」 row · the name is client step) · when called = the same occasions as the library list + after the agent list changes · it is not put on the registry broadcast (the profile edit port runs inside the library write lock, so broadcast → ⛔ computation → read lock on the same thread = deadlock) · no new broadcast (「감시 · 방송 없음」 as it is) — a change inside Engram goes straight into the memory `Catalog` and is seen at the next occasion · an outside change is seen only on refresh · as a block at launch (user 「engram이 할때는 갱신하고, 바깥은 실행할때 막히거나 새로고침으로 … 라이브러리도 동등한 룰」 · §1-3 「트리 ⛔」).
19. 「확인함 — 문제 없음」 The lock order = asset-library lock → {registry · session table · reservation table} in one direction, and the manager calls `check` without those three and without the name-assignment gate (§3) · `register_for_spawn` → `epoch_for_spawn` stays attached (`manager.rs:1234-1270` — the preprocessing is between 「없을 때 넣기」 and the update) · the preprocessing does not use the session id (CLAUDE.md 「세션 id 영속」) · it is correct that `SpawnByCwd` calls `spawn_agent` from a tokio worker (`connection_core.rs:245`).

### 1-12. Third-party libraries

| Positioning (Floating UI) | Re-confirm — client step (0a 2026-10-31) | `@floating-ui/react-dom` 2.1.8 (an indirect dependency Radix pulls in now — `package-lock.json`) | Detail card flip → shift → clamp | `DetailFlyout` | The rule (§1-10 `DetailFlyout` · D11 confirmed 2026-10-18) brings in flipping, so the old 「슬롯 사각형으로 충분 — 안 씀」 is stale. Whether to write it by hand or to raise this to a direct dependency is chosen at implementation (raising it makes it a new direct dependency — a T-62 target) · the 0a research conclusion = `DetailFlyout` is `@floating-ui/react-dom` direct (latest 2.1.9 · the order of the flip → shift → clip middleware is set directly) · Radix Popover only if it passes the same ladder test (research = `docs/research/frontend-popover-query-modal-markdown-2026-10-31.md`) |

When a new dependency comes in, it is a regeneration target of T-62 (the license notice file).

**Client step material (0a 2026-10-31 — nothing is chosen here · G20)** — research conclusions for parts not in the table: modal = `@radix-ui/react-dialog` 1.3.1 (focus trap · aria · Esc · portal — the stacked-layer Esc regression #4143 is tested on the installed version · runner-up native `<dialog>` — it has `cancel` · `close` events and CSS animation) · blocked-launch window = a separate Tauri window as this TRD says (owner = the main window) · markdown preview = reuse react-markdown ^9.1.0 · without rehype-raw (the trust boundary is not only raw HTML but also the configured plugins · components · URL transform). The four client-step spikes = cross-window HTML5 drag · shared react-dnd manager + custom adapter · the compatible combination of react-dnd 14/16 · arborist 3/4 · marquee versus virtualization (「공백 · 실측 필요」 of the two reports).

## 2. User decisions

### 2-1. PRD recommended values this design relies on (places to fix if they change)

| PRD | Value this document assumed | If it changes |
|---|---|---|
| D1 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommended value as is)** — the instructions of a nested preset come **first** | One place, the `resolve.rs` expansion order |
| D2 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommended value as is)** — the preset area is also reordered by dragging | The draggable-area list of `EditorSection` · the `section` value of `preset.reorder` |
| D3 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18** — working folder = an agent value · seen and changed in the agent slot's 「작업 폴더」 area (typing · browsing) · locked after a conversation · while running (two lock wordings, one per reason — P2-REVISED) · LLM = `agent.setWorkspace` (a preset does not carry it · creation without a path · automatic path = next topic §8) | Profile `cwd` · `WorkspaceField` · `agent.setWorkspace` |
| D4 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18** — at creation only the default preset · backend · mode = agent values · no migration of existing agents (user 「기존거 필요없어」 · the old 「Δ1 — 기본 + 기본 제공 백엔드 프리셋」 and migration removed) · the creation flow stays as it is now (P2-REVISED) · the old `agents.json` is neither read nor deleted — the new edition uses a new profile file (G32 「옛것은 물어보지마 현재 기준으로 그냥 다 날리면됨」 — replaces the old 「백업 · 빈 명부」) | Profile constructor · profile file name (§1-5 「옛 에이전트」) |
| D5 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18** — backend · mode = agent values · fixed at creation | `AgentCommand` as is |
| D6 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18** — effort = the backend raw-text field (the model too) | — |
| D7 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommended value as is)** — in-file `kind` marker · folder magnifier = filter | Kind decision · magnifier behavior |
| D8 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommended value as is)** — the 「기본」 area stays empty and can be re-attached from there too (in addition to the 「기본 제공」 list of the `+` search) | `PresetEditor` default area |
| D9 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18** — the four session resolutions as they are · the 「잠김」 of ③ is dropped (the JSON selection field is not locked — §1-5) | Agent preset slot shell (edit component only — B15 · the old `LibrarySlot` `editor-only`) · constructor · JSON selection field lifetime |
| D10 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 = confirm once → revised 2026-10-19 = no candidate suggestion** (convention — peers make the user find the first one by hand and link only the rest by name · ★reverses memo 68 「저절로」★ · B11 · user 2026-10-19 「ㅇㅇ 그게 더 자연스러운것같네」 — the old 「훑기 때 후보 = 제안(누르면 적용) · 막힘 ④ 다시 연결 확인 대기」 removed · PRD D10's 「리뷰 때 확인」 is also closed by this) — missing row = 「찾을 수 없음 · [다시 연결] ×」 · [Relink] = the same kind only · a popover with the old name filled in → choosing one links the rest automatically (all of the same old path · the same name in the same old folder · 「N개 더 찾아 연결함」) · nothing is linked before choosing · 1st edition (P1) · the rest of the same old folder is the same kind only (M14) | Flow ② · `library.relink` · `relink.rs` — the switch `RelinkMode` · the index `index.rs` was removed |
| D11 | **Confirmed 2026-10-18 (convention)** — the detail card is not clipped: agent slot = outside right → outside left → inside the window (flip → shift → clamp) · library 1~4 = from the left edge of the right-hand area over the list · replaces the old recommendation 「전부 창 밖일 때만 슬롯 안」 (reverses memo 137 「뒤집기 · 안쪽 대체 없음」) | One place, the `DetailFlyout` position calculation |

### 2-2. TRD-step decisions (each with a recommendation + a one-line reason)

- **T1 Fetching slot data = TanStack Query** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommendation as is)** · confined to the `libraryQueries.ts` single file. Reason: it gives request type · focus invalidation · reflecting edit replies · merging duplicate requests as they are (the `/research` the memo planned was not run — it may be confirmed at implementation).
- **T2 Hand-written comments in preset files** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 = keep them** (convention — user 「그런 라이브러리를 찾고 없으면 관행대로 처리해」 · reverses the old recommendation 「사라짐을 받아들이고 머리 주석 · 메모는 `description`」). Ground = if a tool that edits a user's file wipes out comments, complaints follow (Home Assistant · openHAB) · round-trip preservation is the reason ruamel.yaml, the de facto standard on the Python side, exists (`docs/research/yaml-comment-preserving-edit-2026-10-18.md`). Candidate `yaml-edit` 0.3.2 (rowan lossless — map set/remove/rename · list push/set confirmed in the docs · list insert/remove/reorder · comments of a removed item unconfirmed) → **first implementation step = a spike with real preset files · compare against `yamlpatch`** (§5 0b). If neither works, fall back to the old recommendation (accept the loss + the memo goes to `description`) and report to the user. Reading stays serde-saphyr — only the edit write path changes. **→ 0b finished 2026-10-31** (main M61): `yaml-edit` `=0.3.5` stands with three conditions (§1-4 `format.rs`) — no fallback · `yamlpatch` rejected · the remaining user share = §6-5 6 (the comment on the line above an item being removed) → **closed 2026-10-31 (user G48 — accepted as known · comment preservation is as much as that library does · no extra effort)**.
- **T3 Two or more `model` fields** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 = no check** (user 「클로드 쪽에서 허용하면 허용인거고 안되면 충돌인거지」 · 「대조표? 매번 업데이트 받아서 비교된다는거잖아. 그게 아니라 이번에 적용하고 나중에 또 대응해야되면 빼는게 맞고」). The formal `model` field is gone, so the model is inside the raw-text field and Engram does not interpret the raw text — if two settings put a model in the same field, it is whatever the CLI does. The old recommendation (conflict) was removed because checking would require fixing a per-CLI comparison table at every CLI update. What was accepted = which model it comes up with is not shown on screen (§6-3).
- **T4 Default location of the Engram folder** — deferred (next topic — the agent tree · moved to §8 with the recommendation as is · deferral kept on user 2026-10-18 as well).
- **T5 There is no settings window in the code** (R9 · E3) — **confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommendation as is · wording confirmed by the user 2026-10-18)**: the first edition is the tree header (the 「…」 menu beside the path → 「라이브러리 폴더 바꾸기…」 → the folder picker window — R9 · G2 · user 2026-10-20 「A 로하자. 라이브러리 폴더 바꾸기도 메뉴에서 선택하는게 더 명확하니. 나중에 메뉴 많고 세분화 되면 다른 방향으로 생각해보던가」 — reverses the old 「바로 폴더 선택 창 · 메뉴 없음」 (2026-10-16 · wording confirmed 2026-10-18) · that menu also contains ☐ 「기본 제공 보기」 — §1-10 `LibraryTree`) · commands (`library.setRoot` · `settings.set`) only · the settings window is a separate task. Reason: a settings window is a screen design wider than this feature.
- **T6 Changed instructions on a codex JSON resume** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommendation as is)**: only when the instructions hash changed, on the first turn use the experimental API `additionalContext` to 「바뀌었다」 + the new text (inside backend/codex). Reason: the only measured way to keep R36 on codex (the official `developerInstructions` is silently discarded on a resume). Alternative = not reflected until a new conversation + a warning. The place of the last-applied hash = the profile field (§1-5 · M16 · review #18).
- **T7 How claude skills are carried** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 (recommendation as is)**: `--plugin-dir` (name `engram:<스킬>` — e.g. `engram:review`). Alternative = `--add-dir` (no prefix · if a name overlaps a native skill, the winner is unknown). Reason: it does not mix with the user `~/.claude/skills` and loading was measured.
- **T8 JSON-mode model · effort selection field** — **handed over to the next topic (the agent system) 2026-10-20** (G10 · user 「에이전트 쪽 연결은 프리셋 꽂는거에 집중하고 이번에 다 끝나고 에이전트 시스템 바로 이어서 할건데 그때 안건 꺼내서 하면 될듯」 — the old 「이 기능의 마지막 단계(§5 14 · 2판 — 범위 안이다 · B16 · 「다음 주제」 is only the working-folder share)」(confirmed 2026-10-18 · 2026-10-19) is reversed · the old reason 「프리셋 없이도 서는 독립 부분이다」 supports the handover). It is not built in S25, and the spec below is picked up by that topic (the same topic as the working folder item §8). It is not in the code (R35 is written as if it were). Spec — the list = only the models of that agent's backend (the backend is fixed per agent — two backends are not mixed in one list · B16). It neither blocks nor locks (the value the CLI started with is preselected · whichever source the first message comes from, that value — §1-5), it is for that session only, and both model and effort reach the session by a backend request before the first message. **A measured confirmation on each of claude and codex is the acceptance gate of that step**, and if it fails, implementation stops and returns to the user (an R35 amendment or another way) — it does not silently turn the field off or restart. **Open** = the place inside the chat slot (the main-session proposal = the 「모델 · 강도 ▾」 button on the input side · undecided).
- **T9 Re-injecting instructions after codex compaction** — **dropped to a follow-up 2026-10-18** (user 「codex 압축은 나중에 고려하자. 모든 유저 고려하면 넣는게 맞겠지만 후속으로 빼자.」) — excluded from the first edition. **Premise corrected by step 6** (M8 — mock provider, manual compaction, codex 0.163.0): the earlier reading "compaction discards the developer message" does not hold for the instructions given at start — a compaction in the process that started the thread keeps the start instructions, and since step 6 every `thread/resume` carries `developerInstructions` (codex ≥ 0.155.0), so a compaction in a resumed process keeps them too (before that fix it left a resumed thread with no instructions at all). What a compaction still drops is `additionalContext` (the changed-instructions note of T6) and anything sent only in history. Still unmeasured: automatic compaction and a real server's remote compaction item. Reason it stays a follow-up: re-sending what a compaction drops needs a separate path, compaction detection + `inject_items` (an experimental API). The added measurement item = whether codex skills (JSON mode `extraRoots`) survive compaction (M8 saw the skills message rebuilt after a mock compaction — likely; a real server is the step-8 lane — §6-2 · the old item's terminal `AGENTS.override.md` was removed by P4-FINAL, so there is nothing to measure — the codex terminal carries neither preset instructions nor skills).
- **T10 Merging the preset claude raw text into a single `--settings` file = replacing ADR-0109 decision 9 (「조각은 `allowedMcpServers` 단일 키」)** — **confirmed 2026-10-18 = no hidden settings** (user 「프리셋으로 해줘. 사실 감추는 설정이 있으면 안되지.」 — the old recommendation 「`allowedMcpServers` 는 Engram 값이 권위 · 프리셋의 그 키는 버리고 경고」 removed). The Engram fragment `allowedMcpServers:[{serverName:"engram"}]` is moved into the claude raw text of the built-in 「기본」 (beside turning off auto-update · §1-2), and all attached presets' claude raw text is merged into one file in the order attached and passed as a single `--settings` — values: the later wins · lists: merged (visible behavior · no Engram-first rule · merge rule = §3 · user 2026-10-18 「이어붙이기 진행」). Detaching 「기본」 may also drop the Engram channel, so 「Engram 메시지 · 제어 통로」 is added to the detach confirmation wording. A second `--settings` stays rejected because its behavior is not in the docs (unmeasured). `--mcp-config` (registering the Engram MCP server) is out of scope — the launch arguments stay as they are (they are arguments that carry the address · token on every launch, so not settings — user confirmation 2026-10-18).
