### 2-4. Open items (what this revision left)

1. **How to find the built-ins (M7)** — release build = `builtin\` at the install location (the daemon passes it as a value — §1-1) · where development · test · `ENGRAM_DATA_DIR` look · the step that ships it in the release zip — no rule has been set (it is set before that step · §5).
2. **When to withdraw the old priming path** — the control channel's `priming_file` (`ENGRAM_PRIMING_FILE` · `prompts/agent-priming.md` — ADR-0092) is used only by `NoopLaunchKits` (§1-5 (나) 4). Not decided: whether to withdraw that path once real materials are plugged in · how to keep the Noop regression golden (ADR-0092 is amended, not discarded — only decision 1's fixed path · §7 35 · user 2026-10-31 G46).
3. ~~**Order scope of backend tags (M20)**~~ — **Closed again 2026-10-31 afternoon (user G51)**: per preset — one preset at a time in expansion order, its common items first, then that preset's backend items (§1-3 1 · reverted to the first reading of M20 「프리셋 하나 안에서」 · reverses the earlier closure 「펼친 전체에 걸친다 — 모든 공통 항목을 펼친 순서로, 그다음 그 백엔드 항목 전부」 (M24-4 · M25)).
4. **Skill conflicts and tags in a single preset (no agent)** — if a claude-tag skill and a codex-tag skill have the same name, is that a conflict in the edit area (they are never loaded onto one agent together)? Not decided (§1-3 5).
5. ~~**Characters for the skill name check (M13)**~~ — **Closed 2026-10-31 (M24-3 · M25)**: the mismatch is intentional — the conflict key keeps `_` (all backends), while the 1–64 character `[a-z0-9-]` name check is an 「오류」 (error) only on the backend that rejects it (codex JSON) (§1-3 5 · backend settings-check port).
6. **Old failure display for a blocked launch (`failed_reason`)** — overlaps with P8's live ⛔ — whether to keep it for blocks too (§1-5 (가) 1).
7. **Keeping sync (async import) gate · HTTPS client (M17 · §1-12)** — **moot: ADR-0359 dropped the schema fetch (step 19).** Was: decided when the schema fetch comes in; client = ureq 3.5.0 blocking (0a 2026-10-31 — runs on one daemon thread without tokio · its relation to the gate is checked at step 19 · §1-12).
8. **Final wording of the G28 notice** — shown to the user at implementation time (draft = §1-3 6). Step 16 (2026-11-05) ships the generic text `알림(<code>): 의도된 동작입니다. 대상 — v1 · v2` for every code (`launch::notice_text` — control characters escaped; shown to the user, no objection). The notice codes and their per-code wording are owned by the steps that produce the notices (6 · 7 — codex terminal omissions · dim lines · the folder line) and are shown to the user there. **Decided at step 6** (user 2026-11-09, `(으)로` 2026-11-10): `codex-terminal-omitted` · `codex-terminal-folder-omitted` · `codex-same-name-skills` carry their own wording in `launch::notice_text` (`trd-codex-translation.md` §4-9 · PRD: the two terminal codes in the R37 addendum, `codex-same-name-skills` in R57); every other code keeps the generic text. Still open: the dim lines and the folder line wording (steps 7 · 13).
9. ~~**Fake prototype that builds only the screen first (G20)**~~ — **Closed 2026-10-31 (M25)**: not built — the client is built against the real daemon after the server steps (G20).

## 3. Internal choices I made (report)

- **Localization (PRD R49 · 2026-10-16)** — screen strings go only through `src/i18n` keys (`t()` · `ko.ts`). Daemon → screen reasons · statuses are answered in the `{code, args}` form (e.g. `{code:"conflict.skill_name", args:{name, items:[{path, via:[프리셋 경로…]}]}}` — a conflict line lists **all** involved items together with the path each came through · not pinned to two · §1-3 5) and the screen draws them with `t(code, args)` — this applies to `Resolution` · `LaunchBlocked` · `CONFLICT` replies · relink display · the notice list `notices` (§1-3 6 · M19). The error reply of a blocked launch carries the same `{code, args}` in the argument field of `CommandError` (M19 — command crate change) · CLI block reply = a fixed code per reason + arguments + a non-zero exit code (M14 · M19 — acceptance criterion). Human-readable CLI output stays Korean as it is now (policy decided in T-68). Built-in preset display name = screen key, stored value = `engram:/…`.

Where the body has the grounds, only a pointer to its location is given.

- **Command declaration = agent crate block + port** (§1-5) — `usage.*` precedent · `LibraryCommandHost` = a typed method per command (M18). A daemon-own block + table composition was rejected.
- **Complete and use the webview → daemon bus round trip** (§1-7) — no separate wire messages are made for the library.
- **Launch preprocessing = after reservation · before session derivation** (§1-5 (가)) — queued per agent, the registry change is one mutate (compare-and-set) so it does not overwrite a value changed in between, and the latch (ADR-0226) and the incarnation marker see only the matched profile. A block also cuts **before** the control channel is issued.
- **Launch materials = `check` → `prepare` + backend `materialize`** · **dedicated RAII guard** (separate from `ProvisionGuard`) · **placement port `LaunchFs`** = `write_file` · `link_dir` · `exists` · `is_link` (§1-5 (나) 6 · backend placement can be tested without a disk · there is nothing to write in the working folder, so no removal method is provided — P4-FINAL · M18).
- **Pass-through arguments = `command.extra_args` exactly as now** — the backend does not change, so there is no reason to split them per backend (a consequence of the user's 2026-10-18 decision — the old `backend_args` · 「`command` 는 해석에서 파생」 · `backend::command_for` were withdrawn).
- **Instructions are merged into one file** — so there is no need to ask whether `--append-system-prompt-file` can be repeated.
- **claude `--system-prompt-snapshot off` goes only to real materials** — so that resume sees the changed instructions (no cache loss if the text is the same — measured). The argv of `NoopLaunchKits` stays byte for byte (regression golden).
- **claude settings merge** (this rule became a user decision — 2026-10-18 「이어붙이기 진행」 · T10 · §6-5 closed) — in attach order (「기본」 first · then the attached presets — §1-3 1): objects = merged recursively · lists = concatenated with identical values collapsed to one · any other value = the later one. No key is treated specially — the old 「`allowedMcpServers` 는 Engram 값이 권위」 was withdrawn (T10 · no hidden fragment). Consequence = a later preset cannot remove `engram` from the `allowedMcpServers` of 「기본」 (in effect the Engram channel stays — accepted). This is claude's part — codex's `-c` lines for the same key are left to whatever the CLI does and were not measured (§6-3). The owner of the merge rule = the backend settings-check port (claude — §1-5 · M18) · ~~the merged result is also checked against the schema and an error is attached to the line of the later asset that brought it in (M14)~~ (dropped — ADR-0359).
- **Old `agents.json` = not read — the new edition uses a new profile file name** (G32 left the simplest discarding route to the main session · main-session internal choice 2026-10-31) — there is no edition marker to tell old-edition files apart, no setting aside, no separate copy, and rolling back and forward again does not empty the new edition's list (§1-5 「옛 에이전트」 · 「호환 · 내림」). Same route as `presets.json` (ADR-0264 「옛 데이터는 버린다」). It replaces the old 「백업 파일 · 빈 명부」 (internal choice of 2026-10-18). The file name is an implementation judgment — but a `.gitignore` line that blocks that name (tail `*`) is added in the same step (plain `env` · ADR-0264 decision 7 · review 2026-10-31).
- **Overwrite order** — codex follows the ADR-0216 discipline (pass-through → preset raw text (including the model line) → Engram, with a warning when overridden) · claude follows ADR-0094 (`--allowedTools` at the very end).
- **The launch-origin argument and the profile-change field are not put into this edition's port** (M16 · review #16) — a block is the same no matter who launches, and there is no unattended default to fill, so there is nowhere to use them. The next topic (§8) adds them together with that feature.
- **Relink = one apply function (`relink` — all the same old paths + the rest of the same kind in the same old folder — M14) · no candidate search at scan time · no index · no deletion record · edition 1 (P1)** — as a consequence of withdrawing the candidate suggestion (B11 · user 2026-10-19), 「찾을 수 없음」 is now decided only by a `Catalog` lookup (§1-1 withdrawn row — this consequence judgment is for the main session to confirm). The reference fix of the apply step is the same function as file operations (flow ④) (`refs.rs`) · the cleanup after an in-app operation cut off midway is also this (G38).
- **Ship in two editions** (B17 · main-session decision 2026-10-19 — delegated by the user 「너가 알아서. 급한건 아니니 효율적인 방향으로」) — edition 1 = library read · edit · attach · launch · settings check · block · 「기본」 · duplicate · relink (P1) / edition 2 = file operations (move · rename · delete · copy — P6) · trash · drag-move (favorites and their catch-up material were left out of this edition — G34 · T8 was handed to the next topic on 2026-10-20 — G10 · §2-2). Order within an edition: server first (G20 — §5). Reason = keep the review · QA loop small · 「프리셋을 붙여 에이전트 실행」 can be used first · meanwhile file operations are done by Explorer · the LLM. Boundary = §5.
- **Agent slot = only edit components + data hooks are mounted** (B15 · main-session decision 2026-10-19 — delegated by the user 「알아서 해 이 부분은」) — the tree · list · filter · react-arborist · virtual table are not mounted (§1-10).
- **Duplicate name = the app's existing name-issuing rule 「<이름>(2)」 · (3) …** (M12 · review #13 — no space · ADR-0115 · ADR-0120 · ADR-0123 · issuer `manager.rs:1215`) — the old 「<이름> (2)」 (B13 · R17 numbering rule for new item names) made the second duplicate 「bob (2)(1)」, so it was withdrawn (§1-5 「복제」).
- **Excerpt generation = asset-library** — from the Markdown AST, remove code · tables · HTML · front matter and keep only the text (strip-markdown style · parser = pulldown-cmark 0.13.6 — everything under the front-matter `MetadataBlock` is explicitly skipped and tables turn on `ENABLE_TABLES` · 0a 2026-10-31 · §1-12). It goes into the instruction description field of the `library.list` row (the one line of a 「+ 추가」 search result line) — not on the detail card. Rules = PRD R15 · survey = docs/research/markdown-plaintext-snippet-survey-2026-10-17.md · docs/research/snippet-vs-rendered-preview-placement-survey-2026-10-17.md.
- **`rev` = stable content hash** (M21 — the same value after a daemon restart · across editions · sha2 `0.10` SHA-256 · raw bytes only — 0a 2026-10-31 · §1-12) · the agent attachment's `rev` = the same hash of the attached preset list (in `default_preset` and `presets` order) (M11 — main-session definition · §1-4 flow ③ · the list uses a canonical encoding — length-prefixed UTF-8 + fixed LE u64 + a default-preset field marker, so `["ab","c"]` and `["a","bc"]` do not collide · 0a 2026-10-31). The old 「파일 바이트 64비트 해시(std) · 데몬 프로세스 안에서만 비교」 was withdrawn because it made the first reorder after a restart a spurious `CONFLICT`.
- **Lock = one `std::sync::RwLock` in asset-library** — the port is synchronous, so it is not a tokio lock. Holding that lock, only two edges go outward: the profile edit port (→ manager · registry — M18) and `ProfileRegistry` reads (M5). Conversely, when the manager calls `LaunchKitSource` it holds neither the registry · reservation-table locks nor the name-assignment gate (it calls after finishing the reservation · registry reads — §1-5 (가) 1 · this is a rule — check at implementation that it is kept) — so asset-library → manager is one direction and there is no cycle edge. ~~The schema fetch runs outside this lock (P10).~~ (dropped — ADR-0359) **Do not take the write lock while holding the read lock** (`std::sync::RwLock` has no upgrade — the `Catalog` update of the launch check · list safeguard takes a short write lock after releasing the read lock) · **that late merge is conditional** — inside that write lock, compare the change generation · root generation · swap-in count that were noted down together in the scan-generation `Mutex` before the disk read; if a generation moved, discard the whole thing, and if only swap-ins got in between, discard just the insertion of paths that are not in the `Catalog` (changes — including the settings re-check under a new schema · `library.setRoot` · a scan's swap-in raises its own number before releasing the write lock · review 2026-10-31 R2 · R3) · the scan ↔ Engram-change exclusion is held not by this lock but by the scan generation `Mutex` + `Condvar`, and the two enter in the order of one ticket line (FIFO — a later arrival cannot overtake the waiting side · R2 · a hand-built ticket counter over std `Mutex` + `Condvar` · wake-up `notify_all` — 0a 2026-10-31 · §1-12), that `Mutex` is a leaf (while held it takes no other lock · while waiting on that `Condvar` it holds no library lock — so an edit's first-scan join happens at the method entrance, before the ticket · library lock · R2) — §1-4 flow ① 「잠금」 ① – ⑤ · review 2026-10-31. **Trash `Mutex`** (review 2026-10-31 R3) — queue only the operations that touch `.trash`: the step that moves into `.trash` (delete · files displaced by an overwrite) · restore take this while holding the library write lock (order = library lock → trash `Mutex`), and `library.emptyTrash` takes neither the library lock nor a ticket (`.trash` is outside `Catalog` · the old 「비우기 = 쓰기 잠금 내내」 was withdrawn) — at the start it reads the root once briefly under the read lock (and takes this only after releasing it — the two are never held overlapped), takes this once briefly to copy aside the `.trash` bundle list (if `items` is given, it groups those items by bundle · moving is done holding this, and the listing waits for one operation's moves to finish entirely (for the waiting count of the hand-over below to reach 0) (R6), so the copied-aside bundles have nothing half-full and the boundary of 「떠 둔 뒤 생긴 묶음」 holds — review 2026-10-31 R5), then takes this **per bundle**, sends that bundle, and releases (sending = the platform OS-recycle-bin function waits with a join for the new STA thread it started for that call — that thread knows nothing of asset-library's locks and takes none · if it hangs, this `Mutex` stalls for that long = §6-3 · main 2026-10-31 M64 · review 2026-10-31 R4 — the old 「비우기 = 이 `Mutex` 내내」 made a delete · restore arriving during emptying wait, holding the write lock, for the entire emptying, so the read locks of the launch check · list · `preset.get` · ⛔ stalled past the 7-second deadline) — empty-all sends the bundle folder as a whole (result item = bundle · R5 · user 2026-10-31 G44) and with `items` only the given items in that bundle are sent. **Hand-over** (review 2026-10-31 R5 — `std` `Mutex` · SRWLOCK is not fair, so if it just releases and immediately re-takes per bundle, a waiting delete can lose throughout the whole emptying, and then that wait, holding the write lock, returns to the stall before R4) — the step that moves into `.trash` · restore raise the waiting count before waiting on this and lower it when their step is finished, and emptying, before copying the list and before taking each bundle (including the first bundle after the list was copied · the check before copying the list = R6), waits on `Condvar` until that count is 0 if it is greater than 0 — that wait holds no lock at all (neither the library lock nor this). **The trash step of one operation = all of that operation's moves into `.trash`** (delete · overwrite move · multi-item restore — review 2026-10-31 R6) — raise the waiting count before the first move and lower it after the last (the held state may be set and released per item — even in between the count is not 0, so emptying neither copies the list nor takes a bundle) · one bundle per operation as well. The waiting count must be raisable even in the middle of sending a bundle (if it sits behind a lock held while sending, emptying reads 0 the moment that lock is released and takes it again — the old gap exactly) — an example shape = `Mutex<휴지통 상태>` + `Condvar` (state = held · waiting count · the inner `Mutex` is held only while the state changes, and not during sending · moving · in this shape 「이것을 잡는다」 = sets held · when the state changes, wake all (`notify_all` — there are two kinds of waiters — the movers · emptying · R6) · lowering the waiting count is also done on failure · early-return paths (guard · R6) — the exact shape is an implementation judgment). A bundle made after the copy-aside is not touched, and an item restored out before its turn is not in that bundle folder for empty-all and so does not go with it, while for `items` that item's result is `NOT_FOUND`, and even if `library.setRoot` intervenes after the copy-aside, emptying continues with the copied-aside root's paths (it does not move over to the new root · if that path has vanished, that bundle · item is `NOT_FOUND`). It is a leaf (while held it takes no other lock). The trash view (`library.trash`) does not take it (a read — if emptying is in progress, it sees what is left at that moment). A delete · restore that arrives during emptying waits for this while holding the write lock — with the hand-over this is at most the time of sending the current one bundle, and only for that time does the launch read lock also stall (if even one bundle takes long = §1-11a finding 17).
- **Daemon identity = owned by the daemon · asset-library receives it as a value** — `config.rs` in the old daemon-module era held `DaemonIdentity`, but identity is not a library concept, and this keeps the crate unaware of daemon internals (§1-4 · §1-1).
- **The fetch port is defined by asset-library and implemented by the daemon over HTTPS** (M17 — the user of the port owns it · the daemon is just a download that does not know what it received) · the backend settings-check port only emits the address · version (§1-5).
- **Built-in references = fixed ASCII id** (`engram:/default` etc.) · only the display name is Korean.
- **Default preset = `default_preset` boolean** — a separately shown field that does not take part in the order. Commands also accept the `engram:/default` path.
- **Old `preset_palette` slot = fold into an empty slot** — preserving unknown content is not a device for protecting a withdrawn feature.
- **One more submenu level in the slot menu (ADR-0065 addendum)** — the shape of R10's 「라이브러리 ▸ 1~4」. The flat 「라이브러리 1~4」 was rejected.
- **Per-number view state = the shell is the authority · `libraryView.*` shell command** — fires at the windows that hold a slot of that number, and the selection summary at all windows (⇐ source — it holds among pop-outs too).
- **Local OS action = the shell's Rust through the plugin's Rust API** — does not widen the webview opener permission.
- **Area width = inside the per-number view state · the existing `Splitter` family** — `react-resizable-panels` is not introduced.
- **Saving a screen preset edit = one new bus command `preset.save {path, instructions, skills, settings, presets, folders?}`** (main 2026-10-31 — interface · the route of 「초안 + [저장] · 마지막 저장이 이긴다」 decided by user G52 · G53 · §1-7 · flow ③) — replaces that preset's four lists (including tags) wholesale with the given lists and overwrites without a revision check (if the optional `baseRev` is supplied and differs, `CONFLICT` — the LLM · CLI's part · the screen does not supply it · main 2026-10-31 — M84) · writing stays `format.rs` + `yaml-edit` as is (computes the edit from the file currently on disk to the given lists and applies it — hand-written comments are kept as far as that library keeps them · the same pre-write gate) · root generation = the same optional argument as the other edit commands (M14 · M26) · the save of the screen edit area calls only this, and `preset.add/remove/reorder/setBackend` stays, with its plain meaning, as the apply-immediately operation for the LLM · CLI's part · the LLM calls it through the same handle (CLAUDE.md 「LLM-우선 제어」). Alternative considered = collect draft operations and send `preset.*` in order at save time (then a save becomes 「지금 파일에 합친다」 rather than 「덮는다」 — the shape G53 did not choose). **Leaving unsaved · agent target · disk-change notice** (main 2026-10-31 — convention · delegated by the user G53) = 「변경 내용을 저장할까요?」 [저장] (Save) [저장 안 함] (Don't save) [취소] (Cancel) (Notepad · VS Code · Office — only when a person closes that tab · slot, changes the preset in that area, or presses the ✕ of a pop-out window's app title bar — the human-gesture handler of the webview holding the draft asks (M87 · M90) · closing the whole dashboard (tray 「완전 종료」) · OS close · tab move · LLM close · change · other content changes do not ask (M95) and the draft stays in the shell-side draft file — user 2026-10-31 G54) · attach · detach · order of an agent slot apply immediately (property-panel convention — Unreal Details · VS Code settings UI · the draft belongs to the preset document) · no disk-change notice band while a draft is open (Notepad rule) — §1-10 「편집 초안」.
- **Draft check = daemon read `preset.check {path, instructions, skills, settings, presets, folders?}`** (main 2026-10-31 — M80 · the place where M75's computation happens) — with the same resolution function as launch (§1-3 end 「같은 함수」), it returns the conflicts · problems of the draft list in the same shape as `preset.get` · it does not write · the client does not re-implement resolution (§1-7).
- **Folder entries (ADR-0341) — internal choices**:
  - Preset field = `folders`, a list of absolute path strings with no `{path, backend}` form — fits the list-per-section YAML (§1-2) and keeps folder entries visibly apart from the `AssetRef` lists.
  - codex JSON skills = append `<folder>/.agents/skills` · `<folder>/.codex/skills` to the existing `skills/extraRoots/set` call — it fits ADR-0337's one call per process (each codex JSON agent spawns its own `codex app-server --stdio` — `backend/codex/mod.rs` `APP_SERVER_SUBCOMMAND` in the per-agent command build), the API is stable, and it is re-sent on every spawn, so resume needs nothing extra. Trade-off: codex prunes dot-folders under a root, so the two subfolders are named explicitly, and a skill elsewhere in the folder is not loaded (the same set codex scans in a project); `thread/start.selectedCapabilityRoots` would include dot-folders and plugin MCP per thread, but it is experimental and its resume behaviour is known from source only — not chosen.
  - No junction for folder skill roots — the folder is used in place (ADR-0341) and a junction adds nothing when the root is passed as is.
  - Notice-once state = a fingerprint on the agent profile, compared by the manager — persisted (a daemon restart does not repeat notices), backend-agnostic (opaque `Notice` values), one field like the instruction hash.
  - Missing-folder check = a disk check at each computation, not a `Catalog` entry — folder entries are not library items; a check per folder entry keeps tree ⛔ honest without `library.refresh` — it is expected to be small on a local disk (unmeasured — §6-2) and is bounded by the stat timeout on a network path (§1-3 · §6-3). Each distinct folder is stat'ed once per computation, outside the library lock, with at most one in-flight stat per path (§1-3 「Stat mechanics」), so an offline share costs a computation up to one timeout however many entries point at it.
  - Folder preview parser = a backend settings-check port method over a listing from asset-library — discovery rules are backend knowledge (CLAUDE.md 「백엔드 확장」) and the disk walk stays in asset-library, the same split as the codex skill-format check (M63).
  - Folder instructions for codex JSON go right after that preset's own instruction lines — the per-preset order of R4 · R54 (G51).
  - Folder paths are normalized lexically (no link resolution, no disk access) before `path_key` — `path_key` folds case only, and resolving links would make two spellings of a junction one entry while the backend still receives the spelling stored (§1-2).
  - Folder identity in the string contracts = a `folder:` prefix on the normalized path — a third form beside library paths and `engram:/`, so no consumer mistakes a folder for a library item (§1-5).
  - Multi-folder `preset.add` = all or nothing — hand-typed paths, where one typo should be fixed before any are added (§1-7).
  - `preset.save` · `preset.check` with `folders` omitted keep the file's list — an LLM using the older shape cannot wipe folder entries by accident.

The regression net is not tied to Windows — junction tests carry per-OS expected values and do not use whole-file `#![cfg(windows)]`. Tests are written first at each step (TDD — head of §5).

## 5. Migration order (build · tests green at every commit — it stands wherever it stops)

Each step is not 「자료구조를 먼저 갈아엎고 호출부를 나중에」 (tear up the data structures first, call sites later) but the order **stand the new beside → connect → withdraw the old**. Each step is **TDD** (tests first — that layer's line of §4 · CLAUDE.md 「구현 실행 규약」) → implementation → `/review code` → `/qa` → commit, and for each implementation part, the external libraries usable for it are surveyed before that (0a).

**Server first · two editions** (G20 · user 2026-10-29 「일단 서버 먼저가 맞고」 (server first is right for now) · M24-1) — order = **0 master merge → server steps (edition 1 part → edition 2 part) → client steps (edition 1 screens → edition 2 screens)**. The server steps build asset-library + daemon commands + the `engram library.*` CLI and **verify by CLI** — the LLM path is the default and the screen is a layer on top that can be switched off and on (G22-RESOLVED). Editions 1 and 2 stay as the bundles to ship (B17 — reason §3), but the server steps go on to the client only **after covering the server parts of both editions** (M24-1). There is no fake prototype that builds only the screen first — the client is built against the real daemon (M25 · the order of the old G18 · G18-REVISED was withdrawn). The shape of commands · replies is fixed first by this TRD (§1-7 · §1-8).
- **Edition 1** = library read (tree · list · detail · filter · search · refresh · saving the per-number view state) · create new (folder · Markdown · settings · preset) · preset edit (add via three routes · remove · order · backend tags · the problem list above · component-row press card) · attaching presets to agents + the 「작업 폴더」 field · `agent.setWorkspace` · applying at launch (claude · codex) · settings check · the four block reasons · notices · built-in 「기본」 · agent duplicate · **relink** (P1).
- **Edition 2** = file operations (move · rename · copy · delete → `.trash`) · trash view · restore · human-only emptying · permanent delete · drag-move anywhere.
- Shortcuts · LLM commands (`library.*` etc.) follow the edition of the feature they belong to. **Even at the end of edition 1, build · tests are green and edition 1 alone is usable** — a missing line = 「찾을 수 없음 · [다시 연결] ×」 · in-app file operations are done by Explorer · the LLM (if something was moved from outside, [다시 연결] (Relink) · `library.relink`).
- **Folder entries (ADR-0341) are edition 1** — format · resolution · block ① · claude and codex translation · notices once per agent · folder commands · preview; the editor's folder section and the preview card come with the edition 1 screens (13). No step is added — the work is folded into 16 · 5 · 6 · 7 · 8 · 9a · 10a · 11 · 13 below. Steps 1–4 are implemented; the types they own (`LaunchPlan` · `LaunchArtifacts` · the profile) gain their fields in 16, the earliest unimplemented step. Step 16 is implemented (2026-11-05 — those fields landed with it · 「Step 16 landed」 under §5-2 16).
- **What this edition does not build** — favorites · collections + move history (G34 · tracking T-72) · export that bundles along dependencies · import (G37 · T-73 — insertion position = `refs.rs` reference walk) · fixed identity per file (G38 · T-74) · the T8 optional field (next topic — 14 · G10).

**Numbering** — the old numbers are kept (other places in this document point at them — e.g. 0a · 0b · 2 · 7 · 8 · 13 ④). Edition boundaries are split with a · b, and steps inserted in this revision (2026-10-31) get new numbers **from 16** · withdrawn steps leave their number empty (12b · 14). **The listed order is the execution order.** The old 0 (ADR recording) became 0c.

**Client-touching in the server steps** (M26) — only the minimum mechanical changes needed to get the build standing (withdrawing the old preset-palette usage · compile fixes · the new fields of the widened wire reply are not yet read by the webview). There is no new screen feature until the client steps.

**Stop: user confirmation** (G14 · user 2026-10-20 「진행할때 … 중간중간 어느정도 구현되면 멈춰서 내 컨펌을 받아야됨. 왠만하면 리뷰 QA 끝나기전에 타면 되는데 명확한 것들은 그 후에 타도 되고」) — marked per step. **Stop (before)** = user confirmation before that step's `/review` · `/qa` (default) · **Stop (after)** = a step whose shape is clear, so confirmation after `/review` · `/qa` is acceptable. The confirmation material of the server steps = `engram` CLI output · test results (there is no screen) · in the client steps the stop is the 「러프 먼저」 below. Which side each step takes is this document's proposal, and the main session may change it before that step.

   *What P4b (materials preparation · placement) · step 8 (plugging in the real thing) must keep* (S25 step 4 P4a review · main 2026-11-01) — (a) `kits.prepare` · `backend::materialize` are done only from the plan the preprocessing checked (`LaunchPlan`) — do not re-resolve with the `presets` · `default_preset` of the re-read registry (an attachment changed between the comparison and the re-read would be loaded without a check · §1-5 (가) 3) — any future path that uses `command` · `env` also passes through that plan or compares again (the `command` · `env` of the registry re-read after the incarnation marker go to `build_command_spec`) · (b) `last_applied_instructions_hash` is taken from the launch materials (kit) — not computed from the re-read registry value · (c) the library may change between the comparison and `prepare` — `prepare` is done under the library read lock, and if the original has vanished or changed, it is cut with `LaunchRaced` (not `SpawnFailed` — an attempt to open a process was never made) — **the branch chosen = `PrepareError::Stale` ⇒ `LaunchRaced` · no recheck** (main 2026-11-01 · P4b-1 review). Duty of the original: asset-library must emit `Stale` if the plan's original has vanished or changed (it does not fold it into `Failed`) · (c2) **P4b-2 materials reclaim — the owner is always one**: ① the guard holds the materials until `sessions.insert`, and no step that can fail is put between the insert and `disarm` ② after the insert, only the reaper's `reap_one` reclaims — for the winner filtered by the incarnation marker, after removing the session from the registry, outside all locks, **after** the disposition (lowering auto_restore) · list notification (so that a slow or stuck reclaim does not hold up the ADR-0019 「내리기가 마지막」 and the notification · main 2026-11-01 · P4b-2 review) · best effort (the CLI tree may still be alive even when it reaches here — the early-verdict observer's session `Arc` · a contained pump panic · asynchronous `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE` · a reclaim that never runs is backed by ⑥) ③ the materials port `Arc` reaches the reaper by a path that looks at the last value after `with_launch_ports` (not captured at creation) — **the chosen path = the reclaim key `LaunchLease` that the session holds** (the source · id · marker the guard prepared · no `Drop` — carried on the session and taken out by the reaper from the session it removed from the registry · the type guarantees use-once (no `Clone` · `release(self)` · the session hands it out only by taking it out) · no shared field is kept, so even if the port is changed after creation, each incarnation is collected with the source it prepared · P4b-2 implemented 2026-11-01) ④ the kill · teardown path does not reclaim next to the advanced `revoke` (if it does, only after `session.kill` returns) ⑤ `release` is idempotent · bounded (no retry · sleep loop) · the key is only `(id, epoch)` (`launch\<id>-<epoch>\`) · the junction is detached first · files in use are best-effort (backed by the boot sweep) · it does not take the library write lock ⑥ at boot the `launch\` sweep finishes before the materials port is plugged in · `restore_all` · (d) the step 8 handler holding the library write lock (the attach body of `agent.new {presets}` · `create_and_start`) releases that lock **before** activation — `std::sync::RwLock` is not reentrant, so the same thread would stall at the read lock of `check` · `check` takes the read lock exactly once (taking it twice would deadlock with a write queued in between) · (e) the bus `wake_existing` moves `LaunchBlocked` · `LaunchRaced` to `CONFLICT` before the real materials are plugged in (step 16) — now they are folded into `INTERNAL` and the request number is held (§1-5 (가) 1 ①). (Done in step 16 — `b223449e`.)

16\. `S25: feat(command,protocol,agent,daemon): 실행 답 넓히기` (inserted in this revision · G15 · M19 · G28) — the `{code, args}` argument field in `CommandError` of the command crate (matched with the current 「받은 원문 보존」 rule — §1-8) · a bus `agent.spawn` block = an error reply (the four reasons in the argument field · a non-zero exit code for the CLI) · the success reply `notices` · structured block reasons in the wire-activation failure reply (`AgentEvent::Error`) · `notices` in `Spawned` (field shape = implementation judgment — §1-8). `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays at the 8 raised in step 1 · regenerating the outputs · the webview does not yet read the new fields (M26). Noop does not block, so the block · notice paths are measured with the fake `LaunchKitSource`. The notice wording the CLI prints (G28 — §2-4 8) is shown to the user here. *Stop (before)* **Implemented 2026-11-05** — P1 `b454a359` · P2 `e4c442be` · P3 `b223449e` · P4 `8200eae4` (as landed: 「Step 16 landed」 below).

   *ADR-0341 in 16* — the agent-side types owned by steps 3–4 gain their fields here (the earliest unimplemented step): `LaunchPlan` folder list + codex extra skill roots · `LaunchArtifacts` env pairs · the profile's notice fingerprint (`merge_preserving_live` keeps it · `agent.duplicate` drops it) · the `folder:` form in the `SourceRef.path` and `BlockReason::Missing.item` docs (§1-5). **Notices once per agent** (§1-5 — compare in `prelaunch` · fingerprint = omission identity only (code + canonical `args`, no `line`) · record only when a delivering caller's activation actually started a process (a fresh spawn or a caller resume), as a compare-and-set against the baseline `prelaunch` compared — not keyed on `SpawnOutcome` / `RestoreOutcome` (a moot activation reports `Started` / `Resumed` too) — never on a moot activation, boot restore (`Entry::Internal`) or any failure incl. a post-start `EarlyVerdict::LinkFailed` · `spawn_agent_reporting` returns notices but never records · every launch still logged) lands together with the success-reply notices; Noop plans carry none, so the fake `LaunchKitSource` drives the tests. Notice codes and their per-code wording (the folder line included — §2-4 8) belong to the steps that produce the notices (6 · 7); step 16 ships the generic wording only.

   *Step 16 landed (2026-11-05 — `b454a359` · `e4c442be` · `b223449e` · `8200eae4`)* — four sequential pieces (each builds on the one before): P1 command `CommandError` args slot + control body · P2 agent notices out of a launch + once per agent + the ADR-0341 plan fields · P3 bus `agent.spawn` answers · P4 wire activation replies. As landed:
   - **Fingerprint** = a canonical identity string (`launch::notice_fingerprint` — per notice `code` + canonical `args` (keys sorted · arrays sorted), no `line`; identities sorted, deduplicated, joined by a newline), stored as is and compared for equality — no hash (a std hash is not stable across Rust releases). `Some("")` = an empty set was shown; `None` = nothing recorded.
   - **Record point** = opt-in per caller (`NoticeDelivery::Delivered`) and only when that caller's activation actually started a process — fresh or caller resume — as a compare-and-set against the prelaunch baseline, landing only while that incarnation is current. Never a moot activation, boot restore or any failure. `spawn_agent_reporting` never records. Today only the bus `agent.spawn` is a delivering caller.
   - **WS paths do not record** — the webview does not show notices yet. `SpawnProfile` (`Withheld`) and `SpawnByCwd` (`spawn_agent_reporting`) fill `Spawned.notices`; `Spawn` replies `Ack` and drops its notices (moves to `Spawned` when the webview needs them). Switching WS activations to `Delivered` is step 11's (below) — until then a notice set reaching the webview is not marked as seen.
   - **Answer shapes** — `wake_existing`: launch block → `CONFLICT` + `args.reasons` (`LaunchBlocked::to_args`) · `LaunchRaced` → `CONFLICT` (retry). `create_and_start` (`agent.spawn --cwd`): every failure after the create (block · race · store · spawn) → `INTERNAL` + `args.agent_id` (+ `reasons` for a block), so the request id stays held (ADR-0122). Success → `notices` rows `{code, args, line, text}` (`[]` when none or already shown; an absent `line` goes out as `null` on the bus). Control body (`/control/call` · `/control/agent`) = `{status, code, hint, args?}` — `args` only when present, so a refusal without arguments is byte-identical; a launch refusal (`CONFLICT`, prefix `LAUNCH_REFUSAL`) carries no roster tail on `/control/agent`. Wire: `AgentEvent::Error.launch_blocked` (`{"reasons":[…]}`) on all three activation verbs · `Spawned.notices`; absent = byte-identical old events, `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays 8.
   - **Deferred** — the `agent.spawn --cwd` pre-create check (§1-5 (가) 1 ②) moves to step 10a (TODO in `commands.rs` `create_and_start`); until then a block after the create answers `INTERNAL` as above, not `CONFLICT` with nothing created. *Absorbed into step 20 (landed `0badd007`) — below.*
   - **Notice wording** — no production code emits a notice yet; every code takes the generic Korean text `알림(<code>): 의도된 동작입니다. 대상 — v1 · v2` (`launch::notice_text` — control characters escaped). Codes and per-code wording are owned by the producing steps 6 · 7 (§2-4 8). Shown to the user with step 16 — no objection.
   - **Review · QA** — `/engram-review` code deep per piece (Claude checklist · Claude axis reviewer · codex) PASS; `/engram-qa` PASS 2026-11-05 at `8200eae4` (full regression Rust 5220 · frontend 2760 · tsc · 30 gates · claude lane 10 · mutation 4/4 · GUI smoke). Not fixed (recorded in the commit bodies): bidi/format characters not escaped in `notice_text` · an over-deep `args` would exceed the shell's serde recursion limit and drop the reply (no producer today) · test-only leftovers (a daemon fixture with the old roster tail · CLI stub wording · `LAUNCH_RACED_CAUSE` repeating the Display text).

6\. `S25: feat(agent): codex 실행 재료 번역(JSON · 터미널) + 설정 검사 포트 codex 구현` — JSON = developerInstructions · extraRoots (including skills in nested presets — M13) · raw text `-c` (including the model · effort lines) · resume `additionalContext` (T6) / terminal = the priming of 「기본」 by the current path (`-c developer_instructions=`) + the preset raw-text `-c` · preset instructions · skills are not loaded and go to `notices` · **nothing is written to the working folder** (P4-FINAL — the old 「작업 폴더 파일 · 크기 한도」 was withdrawn). The port's codex implementation = `-c` assembly (dotted key · TOML) · skill format (P7AC — YAML through the parse function handed in · the front-matter serde type is defined by agent — M72 · this step's tests use a fake function · the real parse golden is in 7 — main 2026-10-31 M63) · skill name rule (codex JSON only — M24-3) · command-line guard · 8191-character budget (both modes — M8) · unknown key = ⚠. It may be split in two (JSON / terminal). *Stop (after)* **Implemented 2026-11-10** — `090ed679` · `a3891273` · `abee6ebe` (P1 · P2 · P3, design in the child TRD [`trd-codex-translation.md`](trd-codex-translation.md) — as landed: 「Step 6 landed」 below).

   *ADR-0341 in 6* — codex: JSON = the folder's instruction file joins the merged instructions in plan order (so the T6 hash covers it) · the folder's `.agents/skills` · `.codex/skills` that exist are appended to the `skills/extraRoots/set` roots (no junction · nothing written) / terminal = nothing from the folder · the omission notice · the codex implementation of the port's folder load set (`AGENTS.override.md` > `AGENTS.md` · the two skill roots · the skills under them by codex's root-walk rules with the codex skill-format check as a mark — tested with a fake listing). The §6-2 unmeasured item 「codex behaviour when a folder skill and a library skill share a name across extra roots」 is measured in this step (real codex JSON turn) and the result is written back to §6-2 · §6-3, together with the fallback policy if codex drops, shadows or rejects one (decided here — PRD §6 row).

   *Handed over by step 5 (2026-11-09 · `baf769ff`)* — the codex additions to the port are additive (the claude half does not change shape): a skill-root kind in the folder load set · a malformed-skill mark · a per-version `SchemaRefetch` variant · an `omitted` list for the codex-terminal folder notice. Do not reuse `unsure` for a malformed codex skill (`unsure` = "claude's finer rules not predicted", not "the format check failed"). The registry test (`backend/mod.rs` `each_settings_check_key_is_the_backend_word_of_its_backend`) is pinned to claude (`settings_checks()` has one slot) and grows with codex.

   *Step 6 landed (2026-11-10 — `090ed679` · `a3891273` · `abee6ebe`)* — three sequential pieces (each calls the one before; the commits were held until the user decided H1–H5): P1 the codex half of the settings-check port (ADR-0334 · ADR-0341) · P2 shared + terminal translation (ADR-0337) · P3 the JSON channel (ADR-0337 · ADR-0215). Design, measurements (M1–M10 — `docs/research/codex-launch-materials-measurements-2026-11-09.md`), decisions and the as-landed differences = the child TRD [`trd-codex-translation.md`](trd-codex-translation.md). Nothing plugs real materials in yet — every launch is still Noop until step 8; the Noop argv is byte-identical (`launch_argv_golden`) apart from the two Noop guards below, and the Noop handshake changes in one deliberate row (the resume fix). As landed:
   - **Port (P1)** — `backend/codex/settings.rs` `CODEX_SETTINGS_CHECK`; `settings_checks()` holds two slots. Additive port methods `skill_format` · `command_line_args` · `command_line_fits` · `native_skill_roots` · `root_skills`; `FrontMatterParser` gains `skill` (into the agent-owned `SkillFrontMatter`) and `is_yaml_value` (the question codex's repair asks); `SchemaRefetch::PerCliVersion`; `FolderLoadKind::SkillRoot` and `FolderLoadSet.{malformed, skill_names, omitted}`. The claude half answers each new method in one line. Codex answers: schema `rust-v<ver>/config-schema.json` per CLI version · `codex --version` (the word after `codex-cli`) · `-c` text → JSON as codex applies it (first `=`, trimmed sides, plain dotted split, the value parsed as TOML with `toml` 1.1.2 — new direct dependency, survey in the child TRD §9-4 — else the trimmed text with outer quotes stripped; the `use_legacy_landlock` alias; a later line through an earlier scalar makes a table; codex's structured-feature merge) · `merge` = `None` · unknown keys ⚠ · `loads` JSON = everything, terminal = the priming only · skill names `[a-z0-9-]`, 1–64, codex JSON only · the skill format (a hand copy of codex's front-matter split and repair, YAML through the caller's parser) · the folder load set (§1-5) · codex's own skill roots for the same-name notice.
   - **Command-line guard and budget (P1)** — `backend/codex/cmdline.rs` over the new `platform::shell::text_is_cli_safe` (the private `CMD_UNSAFE_CHARS` + control characters). A preset `-c` line is refused for `% & ^ | < >`, a control character, an odd number of `"` (it would flip cmd's quote state before the priming's `<` `>`) or a leading `-`; an even number of `"` and any `'` pass. The priming value refuses `' " % \`, a control character other than CR/LF, and — only when the text has no space — `& ^ | < >`. The 8191 count is an upper bound for both quoting rules (each `"` and `\` counted twice, the program token fixed at 262 units, `SHIM_MARGIN` 300 kept because cmd limits the expanded `codex.cmd` line to 8191 again — M10a); the control URL is bounded at 128 units. **Two deliberate Noop changes** (user-accepted 2026-11-10, security): a space-less Noop priming holding `& ^ | < >` is skipped with a warning (it used to reach cmd bare), and a control URL holding `"` or those characters is refused (a failed spawn); neither input occurs today.
   - **Translation (P2)** — `backend/codex/launch.rs` `materialize`, every check before the first write. Terminal: the preset `-c` lines + the priming value, nothing written; a plan with instruction sources, a priming the line cannot carry or a line over the budget ⇒ `Err` (U1 (a)); a working folder cmd would change ⇒ `Err` — not UTF-8, two or more `%`, a `"`, a control character, or `& ^ | < >` with no space (a single `%` was observed intact through `cmd /c` and the npm shim — research M11, reported — method and version not recorded; final rule by the user 2026-11-10 under the CLAUDE.md principle "block only the minimum", `9969ac60`). JSON: the merged instructions text (read failure ⇒ `Err` — ADR-0351 rule; blank = none), `instructions_changed` (never true without text), a junction `<vol>\engram\skills\<name>` per skill (the claude layout) and the extra roots after it. `build_spec` real: base · pass-through · preset `-c` · MCP · priming (never `-m`). `LaunchKit.last_applied_instructions_hash` is filled by the manager from the post-`prepare` registry re-read; `LaunchArtifacts` gains `config_args` · `instructions_override` · `instructions_text` · `instructions_changed`; `crates/engram-dashboard-agent/src/launch.rs` gains the three notice codes with the user's wording (child TRD §4-9 — `codex-same-name-skills` says `$<이름>(으)로`, user 2026-11-10).
   - **JSON channel (P3)** — handshake `initialize` (`capabilities.experimentalApi` only when the first turn carries a context) → `initialized` → `skills/extraRoots/set` when there are roots (an error answer fails the link) → `thread/start` | `thread/resume` with `developerInstructions`. On a resume whose instructions changed, `additionalContext` (`engram-instructions`, an English preface + the full text) rides every `turn/start` until the echo of a turn that carried it, and stops after 2 consecutive rejections (a note 「바뀐 지시서를 알리는 내용이 연달아 거절돼 이번 실행에서는 더 보내지 않는다」 joins the failure line). `FirstTurnSink` is `Fn(bool)` — whether the instructions were delivered; the latch keeps the first bool (ADR-0226 / ADR-0231 port — session-id persistence unchanged). `thread/readState/changed` is a known routine notification. **Pre-existing fix (M8)** — every `thread/resume` now carries `developerInstructions` (codex ≥ 0.155.0), Noop and real: before, a resumed JSON thread lost its instructions entirely at the first compaction in the resumed process.
   - **Decisions** — the child TRD §9 (main #1–#17 · conflicts C1–C4 · user U1 block a priming that does not fit · U2 launch with a notice on same-name skills · U3 the notice wording) and the user's decisions of 2026-11-10: H1 keep the hand mirrors of codex code with an Apache-2.0 source pointer (whether that attribution is sufficient = the user, with the responsible department) · H2 keep both Noop changes · H3 the space-aware working-folder rule above · H4 `(으)로` · H5 「지시서」 and no hard-coded count in the context-dropped note · no size cap on the JSON instructions text (codex's own limits) · the retry bound unchanged.
   - **Review · QA** — `/engram-review code deep` (lead · risk axis · codex): P1 3 rounds, P2 2 rounds (FIX → PASS), P3 PASS with a tests-only fix round; the H3–H5 deltas re-reviewed. `/engram-qa`: PASS — full regression (5737 Rust · 2762 frontend · tsc · 34 gates) · claude lane · codex lane (`usage_probe_smoke` 2 · `backend_contract` 11 · new `codex_launch_live` L1–L5, real codex 0.163.0, 2 model turns) · mutation 2/2 · GUI through CDP (a codex JSON agent turn and resume with `resume_instructions = true`; a codex terminal agent up). 11\. `S25: feat(front): 웹뷰 → 데몬 버스 왕복 + libraryClient` — the `CommandReply` branch · `callCommand` · the widened launch answer (block reason · `notices` — 16) are read. *Stop (after)*

   *Handed over by step 16 (2026-11-05 · user 「쭉 진행해」 on main's recommendation)* — (a) **once per agent on WS**: when the webview shows `Spawned.notices`, switch the WS activations to `NoticeDelivery::Delivered`, give `SpawnByCwd` a recording path (today `spawn_agent_reporting`, which never records) and move `Spawn` from `Ack` to `Spawned` with its notices — otherwise the webview shows a set that is never marked as seen and once per agent breaks. (b) **The Tauri carrier drops `launch_blocked`**: `daemon_client/protocol_state.rs` `classify_reply` folds `AgentEvent::Error` into `CommandFailure::Daemon { message }`, and `commands/agent.rs` `forward_to_daemon` passes only that text, so the structured block never reaches the webview — widen the failure or forward the event. (c) **Shell bus `agent.spawnInto`** (`src-tauri/src/layout/`) folds the `SpawnByCwd` reply to the id or a text error — the LLM must get `notices` and the structured block reasons as the daemon `agent.spawn` gives them (§1-5 (가) 1 — LLM-first).

A value saying there is no unique id (0 · -1 — we did not measure any volume that actually yields that value) is not accepted as an identity (`Unsupported`). **The fallback branch was not run** (there is no ReFS · volume that rejects 128-bit on this PC) | certain (route) · fallback · no-id values unmeasured |
