### 6-3. Risks

| Risk | Basis · Response |
|---|---|
| Detaching the default preset drops priming · Engram messages · the control channel (`allowedMcpServers` — T10) · disabling auto-update all together | R32 confirmation window (the wording adds the channel) · CLI `--yes`. The channel is actually cut only when the user's global settings block it with `allowedMcpServers: []` (measured 2026-07-24 — `types.rs` `settings_file` comment). The case where an update broke an install is another session's research (not on this branch) |
| Even if a later-attached preset writes `allowedMcpServers`, the `engram` of 「기본」 remains — lists are concatenated, so a later preset cannot remove an earlier value (other list values · `permissions.allow` etc. are the same) | Accepted (T10 · §3 merge — user 2026-10-18 「이어붙이기 진행」 · §6-5 closed). To turn the channel off, detach 「기본」 |
| If two settings put a model in the same backend raw-text field, the screen does not show which one wins | Accepted (T3 · user 2026-10-18) — checking would require updating a per-CLI cross-reference table with every update. It is whatever the CLI does (for claude, our settings merge is 「값은 나중 것」 — §3 · for codex, the CLI behavior when several `-c` lines have the same key — not measured) |
| (Deferred 2026-10-31 · G34) the old 「이동 기록 커서가 바닥보다 오래되면 그 사이 이동된 컬렉션 항목을 잃는다」 | Favorites · move history are not in this edition — look again when they return (tracking T-72) |
| If an in-app move · rename is interrupted midway (the daemon goes down, etc.), preset · agent lines not yet fixed are left as 「찾을 수 없음」 pointing at the old path (G38 — no progress record · no finish-at-boot) | Accepted (user 2026-10-31 「일단 이건 이대로 두고 나중에 생각하자」 · Obsidian shape) — one relink fixes all lines pointing at that old path (R19 · flow ②) · each file write is atomic, so there is no half-written preset · fixed identity per file = later (T-74) |
| After an outside move, that agent does not launch until the user · LLM picks one with [다시 연결] (Relink) (B11 — no candidate suggestion at scan time · 2026-10-19) | Intended (Δe — we do not silently attach a different file with the same name · even peers make you find the first one by hand) — the block reason says 「찾을 수 없음」. Bus · CLI launches are blocked too — the LLM does the same thing with `library.relink`. Picking one fixes the rest of the same old path · same old folder together, which reduces presses. [다시 연결] exists from edition 1 (P1 · §5) |
| (Withdrawn 2026-10-20 · P4-FINAL) the old 「사용자 폴더의 `AGENTS.md` 가 Engram 생성 지침 파일에 가려진다」 | The codex terminal writes nothing to the working folder — there is no risk |
| The codex terminal agent does not receive the preset's instructions · skills (P4-FINAL) | Accepted (user 2026-10-20) — the launch is not blocked, and the notice in the launch answer (`notices`) tells only the caller what was left out (G28) · the wording states facts only — the user's worry that recommending a fix would let the LLM read and insert that file itself · the final wording was decided by the user at step 6 (2026-11-09 — §2-4 8 · `trd-codex-translation.md` §4-9) |
| Moving to a new edition makes existing agents disappear from the list | D4 · G32 = no migration — accepted · the new edition uses a new profile file name, so the old `agents.json` is neither read nor deleted and stays as it is (§1-5 「옛 에이전트」 · M25) |
| (Withdrawn 2026-10-31) the old 「내림 뒤 다시 올리면 에이전트 목록이 다시 비워진다」 | The new edition writes a new file name, so the list is not emptied by downgrading and upgrading again · what remains = agents created in the new edition are not visible to the old build (§1-5 「호환 · 내림」 — accepted) |
| The old-name fill of the [다시 연결] popover is our convenience with no peer basis — when many share the same name, the filled list is long (the old 「같은 이름이 흔하면 후보 제안이 거의 안 생긴다」 is gone since candidate suggestion was withdrawn · B11) | Only the same kind is filtered and shown, and the user picks (R19) · nothing is linked before picking |
| `.trash` stays inside the library folder using disk until a person empties it · it is not emptied automatically (no 30 days etc. — G11 · user 2026-10-20 「일단 유저가 비우게 나중에 필요하면 추가하고. 윈도우 휴지통 생각하면됨」) · after deleting and before emptying, it is not visible in the OS Recycle Bin (B8) | Accepted — the human empty · restore route (§6-5 2). Empty is a shell-only command not in the agent command table (§7 28 · M6 — the purpose is to leave wrongly deleted things restorable) · automatic emptying = a later idea |
| An agent is also the same user process, so in theory it can reach the daemon WS with the portfile token and imitate the shell-only `library.emptyTrash` · it could also delete `.trash` with its own tools (such as `rm`) | Accepted (M6 · G21 — user 2026-10-29: no devices · exception lists are added for human-only things · same stance as P13's `rm`) — the guarantee goes only as far as 「에이전트 명령 표 · 프라이밍에 없다」 (review #3) |
| Empty · permanent delete is handed to the OS Recycle Bin and the OS decides — if it cannot go into the Recycle Bin, Windows may delete it outright (volumes without a Recycle Bin · with recycling off · items that are too large, etc.) and we do not tell them apart · the 「비워짐」 (`removed`) in the result does not say whether it went into the Recycle Bin (user 2026-10-31 G47 — the old reason separation · guard · platform permanent delete after confirmation (G45 · M59 · M70) · `removed_unconfirmed` (M71) withdrawn · what the OS refused (open files · permission, etc.) is kept in `.trash` and reported — M62) | Accepted (user 2026-10-20 「휴지통 비우면 윈도우 휴지통으로 보내고 안되면 그냥 삭제되게」 · user 2026-10-31 G47 「그냥 휴지통으로 보내고 삭제되던지 말던지는 다 os에서 알아서 하라고그래」 — the emptying target is already deleted and went to `.trash`) — the confirmation modal does not mention the Recycle Bin and asks as a delete: 「영구 삭제」 · 「항목 N개를 삭제하시겠습니까?」 · [취소] (Cancel) [삭제] (Delete) (user 2026-10-31 G50 — replaces the old 「Windows 휴지통으로 보냅니다 — 안 되면 바로 지웁니다」 (main-session decision — say it before, not after) · §1-10 trash modal · §6-5 7). The reason for not using the OS Recycle Bin for delete (→ `.trash`) (silent permanent delete) does not apply here — even if it passes, it is the same as deleting straight away |
| The 「같은 옛 폴더의 나머지」 auto-link may attach a different file with the same name | It runs only after the user picks one by hand (the user pointed at that folder · convention — Premiere · After Effects · Resolve · Blender) · the answer reports it with 「N개 더 찾아 연결함」 |
| YAML 1.1 booleans in settings YAML — serde-saphyr reads unquoted `yes` · `no` · `on` · `off` as booleans (measured 2026-10-18 · `flag: yes` → `true`) | (**ADR-0359**: no schema type check — `strict_booleans` stays, and a `yes` in a boolean field is now the CLI's to handle — claude discards the object silently.) Writing `yes` in a string field is caught by the schema type check (⛔ error — launch block ②), or in a boolean field it silently becomes a boolean. The option to turn it off = `Options { strict_booleans: true }` — turn it on (0a 2026-10-31 · §1-12) · when on, `yes` in a boolean field will not silently become a boolean but stay a string and be caught by the schema type check (⛔) (likely — step 7 test · §6-2) · this behavior is intended (user 2026-10-31 G49 「yes는 글자지 뭐야?」 — `yes` in a boolean field = schema type error ⛔ · line number) |
| codex integer field upper bound — uint16 · uint32 fields where the schema has no `maximum` (e.g. `mcp_oauth_callback_port`=70000) are not caught by `jsonschema` (formats ignored · measured 2026-10-18) | Main-session internal decision (2026-10-18): accepted this time — codex rejects at startup, so it shows up only as the agent's launch failure (exit code + raw stderr) and the settings row gets no ⛔ (§1-5 (가) 1 · user 2026-10-18) (the old 「실행 직전 확인 `codex debug models` 에서 ⛔」 is gone since that check was withdrawn). No custom post-processing that reads `format` is built |
| Unknown claude keys — not reported (user 2026-10-18 — SchemaStore misses many new keys) | A typo key (`permissions.allowww` etc.) also passes silently and claude does what it does. Accepted — look again if it becomes a problem |
| (Moot — ADR-0359) The SchemaStore copy lags behind claude (community maintained — `docs/research/claude-settings-schema-source-2026-10-18.md`) | Accepted risk (user 2026-10-18) — a new key is an unknown key so it is not reported, and a key whose type · range changed may be caught as an error by the stale schema and block the launch (then fix the raw text or wait for the schema update). The schema is fetched again when the installed claude version changes (`validate.rs`) |
| In one merged `--settings`, one preset's type error makes the whole thing discarded (claude — exit code 0 · no log · measured) | **Changed by ADR-0359 (user 2026-11-11): accepted — Engram does not check the value; claude then drops the whole merged object silently, as measured in this row, so one bad value in one asset drops every attached preset's settings and `engram:/settings/default` for that launch (`docs/research/settings-asset-validation-spike-2026-10-17.md`, `-p` mode; terminal mode not measured). The user kept the decision after seeing this: an author's wrong value is claude's own behaviour, Engram checks only what its assembly breaks.** Was: A type · range error of a key the schema knows = block ② (user 「막어」). A mistake in a key the schema does not know is not caught (the two lines above) |
| 「편집 맡은 에이전트에만 쓰기 권한」 (memo 38) | Currently claude is `bypassPermissions`, so there is no means of enforcement — outside this TRD |
| Rebuilding the distribution deletes the default root `<배포판>\library` together with the distribution folder (`scripts/build-release.ps1`) | Intended — accepted (user 2026-10-31 G39 「빌드용이 뭔가 저장될 필요가 있음? 내가 사용할건 밖으로 뺄건데」) — same as `data\` · put the library you actually use outside through changing the folder (R9) · the script is not fixed (§1-4 「루트 · 기본 경로」) |
| If a library skill folder that a running agent sees through a junction is moved · renamed · deleted while running, that session loses the skill, and if it is open, the operation fails with 「열려 있음」 (§1-11a finding 14) | Accepted (user 2026-10-31 G42 「그건 기존에도 똑같은거 아냐?」) — same behavior as the current CLI skill folder · no separate handling · edits to the skill file are visible immediately, while instructions · settings are copies made at launch so they take effect from the next launch |
| If the daemon dies mid-write, `<이름>.tmp<pid>.<번호>` (the temp of base `write_atomic` — M56) is left in the same folder — Engram does not delete it, so it shows in Explorer · git (untracked files) · the next write does not overwrite it either (pid · number in the name), so if it dies several times on the same file, several pile up | Accepted (user 2026-10-31 G40 「남기고 알아서 치우라그래」 · not overwriting = main 2026-10-31 M56 — dying mid-write is rare) — the scan skips that name shape · a person may delete it (§1-4 `store.rs` · research = `docs/research/atomic-write-temp-cleanup-practice-2026-10-31.md` — same shape as VS Code `.vsctmp`) |
| A folder with per-folder case sensitivity (WSL `fsutil setCaseSensitiveInfo`) turned on can hold `A.md` and `a.md` together, but the path key (`path_key`) treats them as one — one is hidden from the list or a reference resolves to another file | Not supported (main 2026-10-31 M60) — the folder flag is not read · the premise is that no such folder is put under the library root (0a research = `docs/research/library-scan-file-identity-path-key-crates-2026-10-31.md`) |
| The OS Recycle Bin function calls `IFileOperation` inside the daemon process — third-party shell copy hooks · an unresponsive network volume can hang that thread or kill the process (a new STA thread per call — main 2026-10-31 M64) | Accepted + to be measured (9b pilot — §6-2) — if it hangs, the trash `Mutex` stalls for that bundle (§3 「잠금」), so a delete · restore arriving during emptying waits while holding the write lock, and the read lock for launch · list stalls with it (same shape as §1-11a finding 17) · if seen, move to a separate helper process with a time limit (it can be killed · on timeout = those items `kept` · kept in `.trash`) — not introduced from the start (M64) |
| The path key (`path_key`) is folded with a single OS uppercase mapping, but actual case-insensitive comparison differs per volume — NTFS uses the `$UpCase` table that was in use when that volume was formatted (old volume = old Unicode version) · exFAT its own uppercase table · an SMB share the server-side rules — the mapping may wrongly treat two names as the same · different on characters where it differs from that volume (one hidden in the list or the reference resolves to 「찾을 수 없음」 · another file) | Accepted as residual risk (main 2026-10-31 M66) — it diverges only on rare characters whose uppercase pairs changed between versions (likely — not measured) · put one old NTFS or exFAT volume among the comparison volumes of the step 2 pilot (§6-2 · §5-2 2) · reading the volume's table to match it is not done |
| YAML shape drift the pre-write gate cannot see (values correct · 2nd spike — main 2026-10-31 M68) — ① appending at the end · inserting in the middle of a multi-line flow list puts the new item on the same line as its neighbor (for a map, `, {…}]` puts it at the head of a new line) ② values changed in place, such as tag values · `description`, lose their quotes (`'claude'` → `codex`) ③ the reorder workaround inside a flow list (multi-line flow · flow-map item inside a block list) loses the end-of-line comment of the moved item | Accepted — ① recorded as known (M68) · ② · ③ also accepted as known (user 2026-10-31 G48 「보통 메모 지킴은 맞는데 그걸 위해 별도로 노력할 필요는 없음」 · §6-5 11 · closes the old 「사용자 미결 U4 — 흐름 목록 안 순서 바꾸기를 거절해 ③ 을 피하나」 (M74)) — the gate compares only values, so it cannot stop these three · the values are correct · basis = `docs/research/yaml-comment-preserving-edit-spike-2026-10-31.md` 「2차 측정」 |
| Claude loads only part of a project folder from an added folder — never `paths:` rules · subfolder `CLAUDE.md` · `AGENTS.md` · settings `env` · `hooks` · `permissions`; agents · commands are skipped under `--bare` (ADR-0341 impact · research) | accepted — Engram composes nothing for claude and has no own folder rules (user 2026-11-04) · the preview (§1-7) shows the predicted set · PRD §6 |
| Claude's collision handling between a folder's items and other sources is version-dependent (2.1.284 drops the loser · ≥ 2.1.309 prefixes — likely, docs; version not re-located) | accepted — upgrading claude changes which one runs · library skills are namespaced `engram:` and do not collide with folder skills |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_ADDITIONAL_DIRECTORIES_CLAUDE_MD=1` is process-wide — a passthrough `--add-dir` also loads its `CLAUDE.md` once a folder entry is attached | accepted (ADR-0341 impact) |
| codex prunes dot-folders under an extra root and we pass only `.agents/skills` · `.codex/skills` — a skill placed elsewhere in the folder is not loaded by codex JSON | intended — the set codex scans in a project (research) · the preview shows the codex set |
| The folder preview is our parser predicting claude — it drifts when claude changes its discovery rules | accepted — the launch is unaffected (claude reads the folder) · the parser sits in the claude backend port, one place to follow claude |
| A folder entry that points inside the library is not rewritten by an in-app move or rename of that folder (not an asset) | accepted (ADR-0341 decision 1) — the launch blocks with ① until the entry is fixed (PRD §4 line) |
| A folder entry on a network share — an offline or slow share makes a launch check, problem list or tree ⛔ computation wait up to one stat timeout (the distinct folders are stat'ed in that computation once each, outside the library lock, and a path already being stat'ed is not stat'ed again — §1-3 「Stat mechanics」), and a slow but reachable share can time out and show ① 「폴더를 찾을 수 없음」 although it exists; a stat abandoned at the timeout keeps its blocked OS call until the SMB timeout (Windows `metadata` cannot be cancelled) — one per unreachable path | accepted — the stat is bounded (§1-3) so no caller waits for the SMB timeout and library writers never wait on a stat; the value is tuned at implementation (§6-2) |
| The claude folder preview is default-only — it does not read the agent's passthrough `command.extra_args`, which can carry `--setting-sources user`, `--bare` or `--safe-mode`; with such a flag claude loads less from an added folder than the preview shows; likewise a preset's merged claude `--settings` carrying `strictPluginOnlyCustomization` would turn folder skills off (research) — whether claude honours that key from `--settings` rather than only from managed settings is unknown | accepted (review round 2 · round 3) — the preview is a prediction for the default conditions (§1-5 「Loading conditions」 · PRD §6) |
| Known overlaps with what the backend already loads — a folder entry equal to the agent's `cwd` project (claude may load its `CLAUDE.md` both as the project and as an added folder — unmeasured · codex JSON gets its `AGENTS.md` from its own project scan and again from our composition — likely), and a folder entry inside the library whose `AGENTS.md` is also attached as a library instruction (a `folder:` source and a library path never dedup — §1-5) | accepted — loaded twice, no extra handling (review round 1 · main ruling 2026-11-04) |
| A resumed codex JSON conversation still recalls skills removed from its presets — the skills it saw are in its stored record, while extra roots are per process and set again on every resume (step 6 M4) | accepted (step 6 — the same class as the research of 2026-10-10) — removing a skill stops it loading, not the model's memory of it |
| Same-name skills across codex skill roots (a folder entry and a library skill, two folder entries, or an Engram root and codex's own skill folders) — codex lists both with no error and `$name` then selects neither (step 6 M2) | launch with a notice naming the duplicates (`codex-same-name-skills`, U2 (b) — user 2026-11-09; produced by step 7) · library-vs-library stays the conflict block (§1-3 5) · the notice sees codex's own folders only as far as Engram can (`$CODEX_HOME/skills`, `<home>/.agents/skills`, `.agents/skills` · `.codex/skills` from the project root down to the working folder — not codex's bundled skills, the admin layer, plugin roots or configured project-root markers) — a hint, not a guarantee (`trd-codex-translation.md` R7) |
| Removing every instruction from a codex JSON agent does not reach a resumed conversation — with no text there is no `additionalContext` and no `developerInstructions` on the resume, so the old instructions stay in its history until a compaction in that process rebuilds the context without them (step 6 M8) | accepted for now (`trd-codex-translation.md` R6) — the same holds for a Noop resume whose priming file cannot be read |

### 6-4. Where the PRD and the code disagree (PRD amendment or user confirmation needed)

- (Closed 2026-10-18) **There is no settings window** — matching the T5 decision (no settings window in the first edition), PRD R9 · E3 were fixed (commands `library.setRoot` · `settings.set` only).
- (Closed 2026-10-18) **There is no JSON-mode model selection field** — matching the T8 decision (last step), PRD R35 wrote 「피커 구현은 마지막 단계」 → handed over to the next topic 2026-10-20 (T8 · G10 · PRD R35 says so).
- **R32 「다른 세션이 claude 백엔드에 박는 `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER`」** — it is on this branch: `3036e3d8` (2026-10-30) hard-coded it in `backend/claude/mod.rs` (`NO_AUTOUPDATE_ENV` — every claude spawn, profile wins, and the version probe). It was not moved to 「기본」's raw text: step 5 D1 (2026-11-09) keeps it hard-coded on both paths until step 8 measures whether settings `env` reaches the updater (§6-1); after that measurement, decide whether the backend drops it (R32 as written) or keeps it.
- **R36 「이어받기 포함」** — claude holds only if `--system-prompt-snapshot off` exists, and codex does not hold without an experimental API (T6).
- **R30 「이미 열려 있어도 하나 더」 + R24 ⇐** — the edit area and the source library slot may be in different windows (pop-out), so the shell has to hold the selection and share it between them (§1-9) — it is not apparent in the PRD.
- **R10 「라이브러리 ▸ 1~4」** — the empty-slot menu is already under 「새 콘텐츠 ▸」, so it becomes a two-level submenu, and the current menu schema allows only one level (§3).

### 6-5. Open items (user confirmation needed)

The remaining user open items = **none** (2026-10-31 — 6 · 7 = opened by the 0b spike · 0a research · 8 · 9 · 10 = opened by re-review r7 (U1 · U2 · U3 — decision record ROUND 8) · 11 = opened by re-review r8 (U4 — same record ROUND 9) · 5, opened by R6, was closed by user 2026-10-31 G45 · 8 · 9 disappeared with user 2026-10-31 G47 · 7 with G50 · 6 · 10 · 11 were closed by G48 (same record ROUND 10) · 12 = M82 of re-review r9 (same record ROUND 11 — main decided and reported to the user → replaced and closed by user 2026-10-31 G54) · before that none on 2026-10-18). **The TRD part left by the 2026-10-19 decisions** — 1 was **decided 2026-10-20** (P5 accepted this bundle-folder shape — PRD R18 · edition 2) · 2 · 3 were **decided 2026-10-20** (G9 · G11 · G12 — the transport of 2 was closed by M6 on 2026-10-21 · the name-collision answer of 3 was closed by M26 on 2026-10-31 · both edition 2) · 4 is a check item. The open items this round left are in §2-4.

1. **Place · name collision inside `.trash` (B8) — decided 2026-10-20** (P5 「휴지통 이름 겹침은 생기지 않는다 — 삭제 한 번마다 새 묶음 폴더」 · the place itself = the fixed `<루트>\.trash\` inside the root — G31) — each `library.delete` (one operation) creates a new bundle folder `.trash\<UTC 시각>-<짧은 id>\` and puts the items inside it **at their original library path as it is** (e.g. `.trash\20261019T031500Z-a1b2\지침\리뷰 규칙.md`). Reason = since the bundle is new for each operation, deleting the same name twice does not collide · the original place stays in the path so restore is a single 「묶음 안 경로 → 같은 경로」 · and there is no separate record file. Compared = put flat and on collision 「(2)」 (the original place is lost). The 「지운 시각」 · 「지운 묶음」 of the trash view (2) come from this bundle folder, and restore (3) moves the in-bundle path to the same library path (G9 · G11).
2. **The human empty · restore screen (B8) — decided 2026-10-20** (G9 · user 「그렇게 하고」 · research = `docs/research/in-app-trash-access-practice-2026-10-20.md` — a pinned sidebar item is the convention · Notion · Apple Photos · Google Drive · apps that keep it only inside a menu could not be found) — a fixed line 「🗑 휴지통」 at the very bottom of the left area (under the library header — the favorites header is deferred · G34 · always visible even when empty · not put in the 「…」 menu — Notion shape) → the center list becomes the trash view (`라이브러리 › 휴지통` · returns with `←`) · columns = name · original location · deleted time · grouped by deleted bundle (1) · right-click 「되살리기」 · 「영구 삭제」 · top button 「휴지통 비우기」 (confirmation modal — same shape as B9). **Restore** (G11 · main-session decision 2026-10-20 — reported · user 「윈도우 휴지통 생각하면됨」) = to the original path · missing parent folders are created · if the same name exists at the original place, the modal 「같은 이름이 이미 있습니다」 [다른 이름으로 되살리기] (Restore under another name) = 「(2)」 (R17 numbering rule) [취소] (Cancel) — no overwrite (overwriting would itself become a delete · the Windows Recycle Bin shows replace · skip — from memory · not confirmed) · many at once · references of a restored item resolve by themselves because the path is the same (only when it comes back to the original place — if it comes back to 「(2)」, the reference keeps pointing at the original place · P3). **Empty · permanent delete** (G12 · user 「휴지통 비우면 윈도우 휴지통으로 보내고 안되면 그냥 삭제되게」 · still human only — §7 28) = hand it to the OS Recycle Bin of the daemon PC and the OS decides (whether it goes into the Recycle Bin or is deleted outright · what the OS refuses (open file · permission, etc.) is not deleted but kept in `.trash` and reported — user 2026-10-31 G47 · M62 · the old reason separation (G45 · M59 · 5 · 8 · 9) is withdrawn) · confirmation modal = title 「영구 삭제」 · body 「항목 N개를 삭제하시겠습니까?」 · [취소] [삭제] (Delete) — it does not mention the Recycle Bin (user 2026-10-31 G50 · replaces the old 「Windows 휴지통으로 보냅니다 — 안 되면 바로 지웁니다」 (main-session decision — say it before, not after) · 7) · the OS Recycle Bin function is in platform only (§1-6 · macOS = trash — later · 「플랫폼 중립」) · how to build it = a direct `IFileOperation` call is the default (0a 2026-10-31 · main M57 — re-measured against the `trash` crate at implementation · user 2026-10-31 G47 · §1-6 · user rule 「구현전에 외부 라이브러리 쓸수있는거 다 조사하고 들어가」). **No automatic emptying** (G11 · user 「일단 유저가 비우게 나중에 필요하면 추가하고. 윈도우 휴지통 생각하면됨」 — automatic emptying such as after 30 days = a later idea). The withdrawn old proposal (2026-10-19) = tree root header right-click 「휴지통 비우기…」 + 「휴지통 열기」 (open `.trash` in the OS Explorer via shell `os_reveal` and a person moves things back in Explorer for restore) · no in-app trash view · no restore button. **Transport of empty · permanent delete — decided 2026-10-21** (M6 · main-session decision — reported · review #3) = a **shell-only bus command** `library.emptyTrash {items?}` (name placeholder — M26 · last line of the §1-7 daemon table) — no new wire variant (§1-8 「라이브러리용 새 wire 메시지는 없다」 as it is) · the daemon rejects what arrives over the control route (`engram` CLI · agents) · not in the agent command table · priming · `engram help`. The guarantee goes only as far as 「에이전트 명령 표 · 프라이밍에 없다」 — we accept that an agent is also the same user process and can in theory reach the WS with the portfile token (no device is added — G21 · §6-3). This replaces the old main proposal (one shell → daemon wire message · 「유일한 예외」).
3. **The LLM's restore — decided 2026-10-20 = open** (G11 · main-session decision — reported · restore is harmless — only empty · permanent delete are human-only) — `library.restore {items}` · the trash-view material `library.trash` (name placeholder — §1-7). Other `library.*` still do not accept paths under `.trash` (flow ④). The withdrawn old proposal (2026-10-19) = in the first edition do not open it on the bus, and after the agent moves things directly in the daemon PC's file system, `library.refresh`. The bus answer when the same name exists at the original place = **closed 2026-10-31** (M26) — a per-item result of the same shape as move · copy (only that item gets a conflict result · no overwrite — §1-7 「공통 인자 · 답 꼴」).
4. **Whether dragging between other windows (pop-outs) works (B6 · check item)** — since the slot is in another webview (window), one drag system may not cross windows (dnd-kit, which drags with pointer events, is probably in-window only — likely · whether the react-dnd HTML5 backend, which uses OS drag, crosses windows is uncertain · neither was measured). Decided by 0a research + measurement (0a 2026-10-31 — neither is proven · HTML5 drag is an OS drag session so it may work, but it is untested · there is a bug report that with `dragDropEnabled false` the source is not redrawn until the end of the drag (uncertain) — client-stage spike). If it fails, **only between slots in the same window** works, and that fact is written in the PRD · screen document (user confirmation 2026-10-19).
5. ~~**Whether to avoid falling through from in-use · permission failure to permanent delete (G12 · review 2026-10-31 R6)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G45 「열려있음 지우지 않고, 휴지통이 없으면 영구 삭제」)**: it does not fall through — OS Recycle Bin hand-over failures are separated by reason: no Recycle Bin on that volume = permanent delete (G12 as it is) · in use (open · locked) · permission = not deleted, kept in `.trash` and reported per item (if sending the bundle folder whole fails, per item again · same separation — §1-4 `store.rs` row · §1-6 · whether the reasons can be separated = §6-2) → **0a 2026-10-31 narrowed it further** (main M59 — failure codes do not separate cleanly): permanent delete only on an HRESULT confirmed as 「휴지통 없음 · 재활용 꺼짐」 · too large · unknown codes are also kept in `.trash` and the original HRESULT is reported per item (§1-6) → **re-review r7 (main 2026-10-31 M62)** moved the HRESULT interpretation into platform (asset-library separates only by portable results · the original code is diagnostics) and opened the case with no 「휴지통 없음」 confirmation signal as 8 (U1), and the approval of the scope of too large · unknown as 9 (U2) → **user 2026-10-31 G47 withdrew the reason separation itself** (hand over and the OS decides — 8 · 9 also disappear · §1-6).
6. ~~**Removing an item from a preset leaves the comment line above it (T2 · 0b 2026-10-31 · main M61 — user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G48 「보통 메모 지킴은 맞는데 그걸 위해 별도로 노력할 필요는 없음 … 어쨋든 그 라이브러리에 맞게 하면 될것같음」)**: accepted as known — no separate handling (comment preservation is only as much as `yaml-edit` does · §1-4 `format.rs`). The old question = `yaml-edit` 0.3.5 (and the `yamlpatch` compared against it) deletes the end-of-line comment of a removed item along with it but leaves the comment line above that item in place, so it looks like the next item's comment (for the same reason, a middle insertion puts the new item under the comment line above the next item) — whether to accept it · whether `format.rs` should handle comment lines separately (research = `docs/research/yaml-comment-preserving-edit-spike-2026-10-31.md` 「결론」).
7. ~~**Wording of the empty · permanent delete confirmation modal (G12 · G45 · after M59 — user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G50 「그냥 휴지통말고 삭제하시겠습니까해」)**: a delete question that does not mention the Recycle Bin — title 「영구 삭제」 · body 「항목 N개를 삭제하시겠습니까?」 · buttons [취소] [삭제] (§1-10 trash modal · behavior as in G47 — hand it to the OS Recycle Bin and the OS decides). It replaces the old wording 「Windows 휴지통으로 보냅니다 — 안 되면 바로 지웁니다」 and its draft 「…휴지통이 없는 드라이브면 바로 지웁니다」 (what was tied to G45 · M59 · 8). The rough screen is shown in the edition 2 screen stage (§5-4 13 (edition 2) ②).
8. ~~**Guard cancel and 「휴지통 없음」 (U1 — re-review r7 · user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G47 「그냥 휴지통으로 보내고 삭제되던지 말던지는 다 os에서 알아서 하라고그래」)**: the question is gone — there is no guard · no-Recycle-Bin decision · separate permanent-delete step, and when it is handed to the OS Recycle Bin the OS decides (an item on a drive without a Recycle Bin may be deleted outright by Windows · §1-6 「결과 가름」). The old question = if 9b cannot find a signal that separates 「휴지통 없음」 from guard cancel, Engram cannot empty the `.trash` of a drive without a Recycle Bin — (a) accept · (b) a human-only 「그래도 영구 삭제」 confirmation (main 2026-10-31 M62 · M70).
9. ~~**M59 scope approval (U2 — re-review r7 · user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G47)**: the question is gone — since reasons are not separated there is no scope of too large · unknown codes (the OS decides whether to put it in the Recycle Bin or delete it · only items the OS refused and that remained stay in `.trash` as `kept` — §1-6 「결과 가름」). The old question = whether to approve the scope of M59, which keeps too large · unknown codes without deleting them.
10. ~~**If the pre-write gate rejects a preset edit (U3 — re-review r7 · user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G48 — main moved 「따로 애쓰지 않는다」 to (a) · reported to the user)**: (a) reject — reject with a stable code (name placeholder) + an argument holding the anchor that was hit, and do not write · until it is fixed by hand, that edit cannot be done in Engram · no falling back to a full serde rewrite (§1-4 `format.rs`). Measurement basis (main 2026-10-31 M68 — 2nd spike 22 shapes × 10 operations): rejections 2/220 (0.9%) — both when editing a value shared by an anchor/alias (in that case whatever is edited also changes the alias side, which does not fit the intent of 「그 항목만」 (that item only) to begin with) · 0/200 for the 20 shapes without anchors · 0 cases of a wrong value passing the gate · the gate must compare values, not the schema (looking at the schema alone misses 3 of the 4 rejections) · (b) a serde rewrite loses all hand comments and expands anchors to save 0.9% · editing after normalization does not remove a single rejection (the rejection shape is not indentation · flow).
11. ~~**End-of-line comment lost on reordering inside a flow list · quote shape stripped by in-place edit (U4 — re-review r8 · user confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G48)**: (a) accepted as known — reordering inside a flow list is not rejected either (the ② · ③ of that line in §6-3 — values are correct · main 2026-10-31 M68 · M74). The old question = the reorder workaround loses the end-of-line comment of the moved item inside a flow list (multi-line flow · flow-map item inside a block list), and values changed in place, such as tag values · `description`, lose their quotes (`'claude'` → `codex`) — (b) whether to reject reordering inside a flow list to avoid losing end-of-line comments (the quote stripping cannot be avoided even with (b)).
12. ~~**Where the edit draft lives · rejecting bus close (M82 — re-review r9 · main 2026-10-31 · within the scope of user delegation G53 · reported to the user — awaiting confirmation)**~~ — **closed 2026-10-31 (user G54 「ㅇㅇ ㅇㅋㅇㅋ … 클리쪽이라서 나중에 펼치면 되고」 · G54-ADD 「ㅇㅇ 알았어 이대로 쭉 반영해봐」)**: it replaces M82 — an unsaved draft = a separate draft file in the Engram data folder on the shell side (e.g. `shell\state\drafts\` · not the library folder · name · shape = client stage) · restored with ● on reopening, restarting or after a crash · erased on [저장] success · 「저장 안 함」 · [되돌리기] (Revert) · it also survives tab moves · bus · LLM close · replace (no `CONFLICT` · `yes`) · only human gestures prompt — tab ✕ · slot close · preset change · pop-out app title bar ✕ (exhaustive — later main M87 · M90 · M95) · closing the whole dashboard (tray 「완전 종료」) · OS close · other content changes do not prompt (● on the next start) — §1-1 · §1-7 · §1-9 · §1-10 「편집 초안」. Re-review r10 NF1–NF5, called up by that branch, were resolved by withdrawal (draft line changes · the step order is looked at again in the client stage). The old question = keep the draft (four lists + unsaved mark + draft number) in shell memory (next to `LibraryViewState`) per library number and do not write it to `state.json` (lost on restart — M77), so it also survives tab moves · attach · detach · a human's close · preset change · OS close · app quit = save prompt (M81) · the bus · LLM's `slot.close` · `tab.close` · `window.close` · `libraryView.*` pick change = `CONFLICT` if it is an unsaved draft · discard if `yes` (same shape as R48) — if confirmed, as it is · otherwise fix that branch before the client stage.

- (Closed 2026-10-18) the old 「§1-3 8 단계 막힘 사유 둘의 갈래가 비어 있다」 — the block reasons (six at that time · five on 2026-10-19 · four on 2026-10-20 — P4-FINAL) are one category and block no matter who launches (user 2026-10-18 · §1-3 8).
- (Closed 2026-10-18) **The list rule of the claude settings merge = concatenate** (user 「이어붙이기 진행」) — one rule with no per-key rules: objects = merged recursively · lists = concatenated in attach order with identical values collapsed to one · other values = the later one. The T10 wording became 「값은 나중 것 · 목록은 합침」 (§2-2 · §3). Consequence = even if a later preset writes `allowedMcpServers: []`, the `engram` of 「기본」 remains (in effect the Engram channel remains — accepted). Rejected = replacing the list (a later preset swaps the whole list). It is claude's part, and codex's behavior for repeated `-c` with the same key was not measured (§6-3).

## 7. ADR proposals (numbers are given by `/adr`)

The numbers below are places in this list and are not ADR numbers (other places point to them with 「§7 N」, so keep them — withdrawn ones also leave their number empty). 30~35 = ADR candidates of the 2026-10-20~31 decisions — the large decisions of 2026-10-29~31 (30~33) are written as `/adr` in the document-reflection step, and the rejected alternatives = options the user did not pick in that conversation · shown to the user after they are written (M23 · §5 0c).

1. **Preset · library = a daemon-owned asset, client request-style** — one library folder (R7 · G36 — 31) · presets too are YAML files inside it · sole read/write by the asset-library crate inside the daemon process (crate place = 30) · no watching · daemon-side features (commands · CLI) are always on (G22-RESOLVED). Rejected: shell scan · notify watch + broadcast · a list inside the preset. **ADR-0061 · ADR-0070 (preset name part) discarded.**
2. **Reference = library root `/` path + built-in `engram:/` namespace** — Rejected: relative to the preset file · name reference · ID file · URI shape.
3. **Launch-materials port (`LaunchKitSource`) + backend `materialize` + backend settings check port** — resolution is in asset-library, and flag translation and per-backend settings knowledge (schema source · raw text → JSON · claude merge · codex skill format · command-line guard) are in backend (M2 · M18 · CLAUDE.md 「백엔드 확장」). Rejected: manager resolves presets · backend reads the library · adding fields to `ControlEndpoint`. **Numbered = ADR-0334.**
4. **claude launch materials = merged instruction file + `--system-prompt-snapshot off` + `--plugin-dir` junction + one merged `--settings`** — Rejected: `--add-dir` skill · several `--settings`.
5. **codex launch materials** — JSON = developerInstructions · extraRoots (including skills inside contained presets — M13) · resume `additionalContext` (experimental) · a skill codex cannot carry = 「오류」 block (no new category — P7AC) / terminal = **priming of 「기본」 (the current route) + only the setting `-c`** — preset instructions · skills are not carried and are reported with a notice in the launch answer (`notices` — facts only · to the caller only — G28) · nothing is written to the working folder · `~/.codex` (P4-FINAL · user 2026-10-20 「프리셋은 베이스때문에 무조건 들어가야겠네. 그럼 안에 스킬이나 그런 지시서가 있을때 경고 띄우면 될듯」). Rejected: **always** generating an instruction file + `.agents\skills` junction in the terminal working folder (2026-10-18 decision — races between codex instances in the same folder · replica blocking · no cleanup · review #1 · withdrawn with P4) · instructions only via a per-agent profile `-p` (P4 — left as a research item of 「codex 가 지원하면 나중」 · `docs/research/codex-instruction-channels-2026-10-20.md`) · not carrying the preset to the terminal at all (P4-REVISED — it has to go in because of 「기본」) · a fix button for skills that cannot be carried · 「고치고 실행」 modal (P7A — user 「너무 요란한데?」 · P7AC moved it onto the existing block route) · changing `CODEX_HOME` · local plugin installation · asking whether to create it for a user folder and remembering the answer (the old R37 answer — withdrawn by the 2026-10-18 user decision · since P4-FINAL the question itself is gone — not a user policy question but an item to sort out as ADR rejection grounds). **Numbered = ADR-0337.**
6. **Editing = the preset edit area is a client draft + [저장] (Save) (save = `preset.save` overwrites the four lists whole · no version check · the last save wins — the Notepad rule) · unsaved mark · the LLM · CLI `preset.add/remove/reorder/setBackend` is the operation the daemon applies straight to the latest file, as it is (only reordering checks the version) · attach · detach · order in an agent slot are applied immediately** (user 2026-10-31 G52 「다 수정하고 저장 눌러야되는거 아님? 물론 저장안된건 마킹되있는게 맞고??」 · G53 「… 그냥 마지막으로 덮으면 되는거 아님? 이건 관행보고 따라」 · an unsaved draft = a separate draft file on the shell side (restored with ● on restart or crash · erased on [저장] success · 「저장 안 함」 · [되돌리기] — user 2026-10-31 G54 · G54-ADD) · when a person does tab ✕ · slot close · preset change · pop-out window app title bar ✕ without saving, that webview shows 「변경 내용을 저장할까요?」 [저장] [저장 안 함] [취소] (exhaustive — M87 · M90) · closing the whole dashboard (tray 「완전 종료」) · OS close · tab move · LLM close · other content changes do not prompt (G54 · M90 · M95) · no disk-change notice band while a draft is open · immediate apply in the agent slot = main 2026-10-31 — convention · user delegation G53 · the shape of `preset.save` = main interface decision §3 · ADR-0338) — Rejected: applying screen edits too as operations straight to the latest file (this item's old decision · memo 41 · memo 83 「+ 담기」 immediate apply — reversed by G52 · the old rejection 「클라이언트 초안 + 저장 단추」 is now what was chosen) · version check at save · merging draft operations into the current file (G53 — 「A가 보는대상하고 B가 보는대상이 다르잖아. 노트패드 룰로 해야될듯?」) · keeping the draft only in shell memory and blocking bus close with `CONFLICT` · `yes` (M82 — lost on restart, and every shell close command needed a `yes` gate · re-review r10 NF1–NF5 — replaced by G54) · keeping the draft only in the webview (lost if the tab moves to another window or the LLM closes it — not picked by G54) · (question gone) the old 「전부 판 확인」 — the 「빼기」 of the stale screen that raised that question is gone because the screen no longer sends `add` · `remove` (the idempotency rule of M24-8 stays for bus operations as it is).
7. **File operations = plan → execute → fix references (no progress record · order only — Obsidian shape · if interrupted, one relink fixes it — G38) · Explorer rules (only overwritten files go to `.trash` — P5) · a move inside the root can go anywhere (one root — G36) · references on delete · overwrite = ADR-0339 (overwrite = replacement · deletes, and renames · moves that make `SKILL.md` no longer a skill, let you choose whether to keep or remove the reference — rule source = PRD R17 · R18 · R48 · §1-4 flow ④) · delete = `.trash` inside the library root (20)** — Rejected: outside cleanup + name relink only · permanent delete · an operation record that writes down intent + finishing at boot (the old `library-ops\` journal roll-forward — user 2026-10-31 「일단 이건 이대로 두고 나중에 생각하자」 · no such peer found — `docs/research/multi-file-rename-crash-safety-practice-2026-10-31.md`) · fixing references to the trash path on delete (review #5 — user 2026-10-20 「따로 처리를 해야됨?」 · P3) · a per-file fixed identity as a relink candidate (user 「나중에 생각하자」 — tracking T-74) · the rejection on the overwrite · delete side = ADR-0339 「거부한 대안」. **Numbered = ADR-0335 · references on delete · overwrite = ADR-0339 (replaces decision 5 of ADR-0335 and extends decisions 1 · 6).**
8. **Relink = after the user · LLM picks one with [다시 연결] (Relink) (same kind only · old-name fill), the rest is automatic (all of the same old path · the same name in the same old folder · 「N개 더 찾아 연결함」) · no candidate suggestion at scan time · nothing is linked before picking · from edition 1 (P1) · the rest of the same old folder is the same kind only (M14)** (2026-10-18 convention · 2026-10-19 amendment B11) — Rejected: applying automatically by name alone (memo 68 — no peer relinks by name before the user starts looking) · suggesting one same-name candidate at scan time and blocking with 「다시 연결 확인 대기」 until confirmed (the 2026-10-18 D10 shape — user 2026-10-19 「ㅇㅇ 그게 더 자연스러운것같네」 · peers make you find the first one by hand) · picking one item at a time (convention = 「N개 더 찾음」) · redirector files · relinking across roots (question gone — there is one root · G36).
9. (Deferred 2026-10-31 · G34) the old candidate 「이동 기록(번호 붙은 쌍) + 클라이언트 커서 따라잡기 — 클라이언트 소유 컬렉션을 방송 없이 고친다」 — favorites (collections) were dropped from this edition (user 「그냥 즐겨찾기 일단 뺄까? 추후 구현하는걸로. 일단 좀 써보자」). When it returns, the starting point = a bounded move history numbered per root + a client cursor (precedent shape = change feed · no fixed file id — G33 · a fixed id issued by the daemon was rejected by the user) · tracking T-72 · the ADR then. The number is left empty.
10. (Withdrawn for this edition — a consequence of the 2026-10-18 user decision) the old candidate 「실행 출처(`Interactive` / `Unattended`)가 대화상자 여부를 정한다 — 버스 · CLI 실행은 기본값을 채워 저장」 — the asking dialog (codex file answer) is gone and blocking is the same no matter who launches (§1-3 8). The next topic (asking at launch for an agent with no path) opens it again (§8). The number is left empty.
11. **Backend = agent value (fixed at creation · a new agent to change it) · settings = only a per-backend raw-text field (no formal `backend` · `model` fields · the model is also inside the raw text) · no model conflict check · built-in = one 「기본」** (user 2026-10-18 — replaces the old candidate 「기본 제공 백엔드 프리셋은 사용자 백엔드가 있으면 사용할 수 없음 상태가 된다」) — Rejected: settings decide the backend · model (memo 7 — an error if there are two · no launch if there is none · changing it starts a new conversation) · a built-in backend preset (it yields · is swapped out at resolution) · two `model` = conflict (checking would require updating a per-CLI cross-reference table with every update).
12. **View-side state (per-number view · fold and unfold) is in the shell, with the daemon identity in the key** (the edit draft file also has the daemon identity + library root in the key — not attached if they do not match · main 2026-10-31 — M92 · §1-9) — Rejected: daemon · webview `localStorage`. (Collections are deferred — G34 · whether to move in-slot view state to the package store is for the client stage — G23-NOTE · 34)
13. **Webview → daemon bus round trip completed — the screen and the LLM use the same command** — Rejected: a typed wire message per feature.
14. **Third-party wrapping = purpose port · component boundary** (walkdir · serde-saphyr → library store · format / tree · list · drag · query → one place each of our own components) — Rejected: a 1:1 wrapper per library. Component choice (§1-12 · §5 0a — no place in this list) = **ADR-0336**.
15. **Slot menu submenu two levels (ADR-0065 addendum amendment)** — Rejected: a flat 「라이브러리 1~4」.
16. **Slot data fetching = one TanStack Query file** (when T1 is confirmed) — Rejected: a hand-built zustand cache.
17. **Launch preprocessing (check · compare — filling unattended defaults is the next topic · M16) comes after reservation · liveness check, before session derivation · registry change is one mutate (compare-and-set)** — Rejected: adjusting in the middle of startup (the latch · incarnation marker sees the old copy) · before reservation (two launches of the same agent overlap). (Working-folder adjustment · Engram folder part = §8)
18. (Withdrawn — a consequence of the 2026-10-18 user decision) the old candidate 「패스스루 인자 = 백엔드별 저장(`backend_args`) · `command` 는 해석에서 파생」 — the backend does not change, so `command.extra_args` stays as it is (11). The number is left empty.

## 8. Next topic (agent tree) — the rest of the working folder (user decisions 2026-10-16 · 2026-10-18)

**What this edition does is in the main text** — folder selection at creation (`cwd` required · as it is now) · the agent slot 「작업 폴더」 field (input · browse) · locked after a conversation · while running · `agent.setWorkspace {agent, cwd}` (§1-5 「작업 폴더 `cwd`」 · §1-7 · §1-10 `WorkspaceField` · D3 confirmed). **The next topic = agent tree design** (user 2026-10-18 「에이전트 트리는 다음 주재로 설계할거야. 묶는 폴더도 있어야되고 아까처럼 경로없이 생성이나 뭐 그런것들 다듬어야됨. 지금은 프리셋 실행을 위한 기본 기능만」) — creating agents without a path · asking at launch if it is empty · automatic path (Engram folder · T4) · bundling folder (the old 「codex 파일 전용 폴더」 was withdrawn — after P4-FINAL the codex terminal writes nothing to the working folder · PRD R34 was also withdrawn — M24-6 · the 「폴더를 담은 프리셋(하위 포함)」 of the 2026-10-16 memo is also looked at again here). Below is the working-folder design that was in the main text up to `/review trd` round 3, moved here (slightly shortened) — it is the starting point of the next topic and is to be reopened by user decision then (pair = the workspace part of PRD R34 · R48 · E15). ★「만들 때 폴더를 묻지 않음」 was a TRD interpretation and not a user decision★. ★The 「워크스페이스가 바뀌면 새 대화(`launched_workspace` · `release_session_id`)」 below was replaced by this edition's 「대화 뒤 잠금」★ — bring it out again only when the next topic opens the route of changing it even after a conversation.

**Ownership · persistence (the part taken out of §1-1 · §1-4)** — the location of the Engram folder (automatic workspace) = daemon · `daemon\state\library.json` (the same file as the library root) · handled by `config.rs` · `library.status` also carries that location.

**Profile fields (the part taken out of §1-5)**
- `workspace: Option<PathBuf>` — working folder. `None` = decided at launch (R34 · D3). On disk the old key `cwd` is used as it is (an empty string if `None`).
- `launched_workspace: Option<PathBuf>` — the workspace of the launch that made the currently stored session — if different, a new conversation (preprocessing below).
- Both go into the `merge_preserving_live` preserved fields.
- Migration — `workspace = Some(옛 cwd)` · `launched_workspace = Some(옛 cwd)` (so the current session can be resumed).
- The original reason for fixing `display_name` — if the route where the name was derived from the `cwd` basename changed along with the workspace (empty, or later filled with the Engram folder), the mail address · tree name would silently change (the fixing itself is done in step 3 of this edition — because `agent.setWorkspace` appears).
- Compatibility risk — what happens if an old build launches an agent with an empty `cwd` was not measured (probably it fails).

**wire · screen (the part taken out of §1-7 · §1-8 · §1-10)**
- `CreateProfile.cwd` becomes optional (the folder is not asked at creation). Wire `AgentProfile.cwd` · `AgentInfo.cwd` · bus `AgentRow.cwd` stay strings as they are and we decide that **an empty string = no working folder yet** (the field shape does not change, so generated artifacts · old consumers read it as it is). The screen draws the empty value as 「작업 폴더 없음 · 실행 때 정함」, and the name uses the fixed `display_name` (it is not derived from an empty basename).
- `agent.new`'s `cwd?` — if absent, the Engram folder (R34).
- The tree-panel menu 「에이전트 생성 ▸」 — it does not ask for the folder at creation, and when the **error answer** of activation (one request — G15) carries `NeedsWorkspace`, 「어디서 일할까요?」 (confirmation window `WorkspaceAsk`) — the same route as this edition's block window (the structured reason of the launch answer — §1-5 (가) 1 · §1-8). The old 「활성화 전 앞 점검 `agent.launchCheck`」 was withdrawn (M21 — the command itself is gone).
- To the agent slot 「작업 폴더」 field (present in this edition), add 「작업 폴더 없음 · 실행 때 정함」 and the fact that bus · CLI launches filled the Engram folder (R31).
- Premise (old §6-4) — R34 「워크스페이스가 없으면」 conflicts with the profile currently requiring a working folder (`AgentProfile.cwd` required — confirmed), so the field change above comes first.

**Block reason (the part taken out of §1-3 · §1-5)** — 「워크스페이스 없음(R34)」 = `BlockReason::NeedsWorkspace` — ask **only on a launch the screen initiated**, and a launch initiated by the bus · CLI fills in the Engram folder and saves it to the agent (Δg). ★This edition has no 「화면이 낸 실행에서만」 branch★ (the four block reasons block no matter who launches · the codex file answer was withdrawn — user 2026-10-18) — when this is built, that branch, the launch-origin argument and the profile change field (not in this edition's launch-materials port — M16 · §1-5) are added together (the old §7 candidate 10). If blocked, `NeedsWorkspace` is carried in the structured error answer of the launch together with this edition's other reasons (G15 · M19 — no pre-check command).

**Launch preprocessing (what was added to §1-5 (가))**
- 1 check — in `Unattended`, if there is no workspace, fill in the Engram folder (3rd delta — saved and shown at the head of the agent slot).
- 2 align — `workspace` goes into the compare-and-set target (if it changes again, 「실행 준비 중 프리셋 · 워크스페이스가 계속 바뀌었다 — 다시 시도하라」). `launched_workspace` also goes into one mutate · `workspace` is filled only while it is still `None` (if a person decided it in the meantime, that value is used) · **if the workspace differs from `launched_workspace`**, it does the same thing as `release_session_id` inside the same mutate and writes `launched_workspace` as the new value (a claude session is bound to the working folder). The Engram folder is decided **by agent id only** (`<workspaces>\<agent-id>` — a name that changes is not used) and if it exists it is used as it is (idempotent creation).
- 3 re-read — the first-submission latch (ADR-0226) looks only at ids that do not diverge from the aligned workspace either.

**Changing the workspace** — this edition's `agent.setWorkspace {agent, cwd}` is in the main text (`CONFLICT` after a conversation · while running — §1-5). What the next topic adds = the argument for creating the Engram folder (old design `{agent, path?, engramFolder?}` · R34). The old design's 「바뀐 값은 다음 실행 전처리 2 가 새 대화로 맞춘다」 was replaced by the lock after a conversation (preamble above).

**Engram folder location (old T4 — recommendation as it is)** — release `<배포판>\workspaces\<agent-id>` · debug · `ENGRAM_DATA_DIR` = `workspaces\<agent-id>` inside the data root (issued by `DataLayout` — the same root-source rule as the library `suggested_new`: release default = under the parent of the data root · otherwise = inside the root) · the screen shows the fixed name. Reason: names change, so deciding only by id makes idempotent creation (reuse if it exists) stand, separates program data from work products, and makes test isolation work with one folder. Command = `workspace.getRoot` · `workspace.setRoot {path}` (new agents only — R34). Since there is no settings window, the first edition was commands only (T5 · conflicts with PRD R34 「Engram 폴더 위치는 설정」).

**codex terminal (R37)** — (withdrawn 2026-10-20 · P4-FINAL) the old 「Engram 폴더에도 작업 폴더 생성 지침 파일 + 스킬 정션을 깐다」 — the codex terminal writes nothing to any working folder, so there is nothing to do separately for the Engram folder either (instructions · skills are not carried and a notice is given — §1-3 6 of this edition).

**Tests (the part taken out of §4)** — migration golden `cwd` → `workspace` · compare-and-set does not overwrite a `workspace` decided in the meantime · Engram folder = idempotent by id · workspace change = `release_session_id` then Fresh.

**What was mixed into the migration order (§5)** — step 3: `cwd` → `workspace` (keep the disk key `cwd` · empty string = none) · the meaning of an empty string in wire `cwd` · the screen's empty working folder display · if `None`, a spawn failure as now (reason sentence). Step 4: Engram folder id idempotent. 13 ④: the workspace of the pre-launch dialog.

**ADR candidate (the part taken out of §7 17)** — launch preprocessing also aligns the workspace, and the Engram folder is idempotent by id. Rejected: deciding the folder by name.
